> DEVELOPER

Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Allow duplicate callsigns in reports

## Context
Currently `signal_reports` has a `UNIQUE (exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)` constraint — same callsign can only appear once per repeater per exercise. User wants to allow the same callsign multiple times (e.g. operator checks in again later with different signal conditions).

## Changes

### 1. Schema: `server/src/db/schema.ts`
- Add migration: detect the UNIQUE constraint and recreate `signal_reports` without it
- Use PRAGMA index_list + PRAGMA index_info to find the constraint, then recreate table via `signal_reports_new` pattern (copy data, drop old, rename)
- Keep all columns, indexes, just drop the UNIQUE constraint

### 2. Server: `server/src/routes/exercises.ts`
- **POST `/:id/reports`** (~line 290): Remove the `catch` block that handles UNIQUE constraint violations (the 409 response with `existing_report`). Without the constraint, inserts will just succeed.

### 3. Client: `client/src/components/BundMode.tsx`
- **`handleBezirkSubmit`** (~line 190): Remove the inner try/catch that catches 409 and patches existing report. Just do the POST — it will always create a new report now.

### 4. Client: `client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`
- **`handleBezirkSubmit`** (~line 131): Same removal — remove the inner try/catch 409 handling.

### 5. Stats query: `server/src/routes/exercises.ts`
- **GET `/:id/stats`** participants query (~line 162): Currently `GROUP BY sr.operator_id` with `COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END)`. This still works correctly — `report_count` will now reflect multiple reports per operator. No change needed.

## Files to modify
- `server/src/db/schema.ts` — migration to drop UNIQUE constraint
- `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` — remove 409 handling from POST reports
- `client/src/components/BundMode.tsx` — simplify handleBezirkSubmit
- `client/src/components/LandMode.tsx` — simplify handleBezirkSubmit

## Verification
1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run build"` compiles clean
2. Enter same callsign twice on same repeater — both reports appear
3. Stats still show correct report_count per participant

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
     3→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     4→import { AuthRequest, requireRole } from '../middleware/auth.js';
     5→
     6→export const exercisesRouter = Router();
     7→
     8→// List all exercises with summary stats
     9→exercisesRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
    10→  const db = getDb();
    11→  const exercises = db.prepare(`
    12→    SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.notes, e.oe_link_enabled, e.exercise_type, e.simplex_bezirke, e.simplex_bl_codes,
    13→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    14→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    15→    FROM exercises e ORDER BY e.date ASC
    16→  `).all();
    17→  res.json(exercises);
    18→});
    19→
    20→// Create exercise (admin only)
    21→exercisesRouter.post('/', requireRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
    22→  const { date, name, oe_link_enabled, exercise_type, simplex_bezirke, simplex_bl_codes } = req.body;
    23→  if (!date || !name) {
    24→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Datum und Name erforderlich' });
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→  const db = getDb();
    28→  const id = randomUUID();
    29→  db.prepare('INSERT INTO exercises (id, date, name, oe_link_enabled, exercise_type, simplex_bezirke, simplex_bl_codes) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(id, date, name, oe_link_enabled !== undefined ? (oe_link_enabled ? 1 : 0) : 1, exercise_type || 'Krisenkommunikationsübung', simplex_bezirke ? 1 : 0, simplex_bl_codes || null);
    30→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    31→  res.status(201).json(exercise);
    32→});
    33→
    34→// Get full exercise with reports
    35→exercisesRouter.get('/:id', (req, res) => {
    36→  const db = getDb();
    37→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    38→  if (!exercise) {
    39→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });
    40→    return;
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  const repeaters = db.prepare(`
    44→    SELECT er.*, r.short_name, r.site_name, r.band, r.callsign as repeater_callsign,
    45→      r.frequency_mhz, r.offset_mhz, r.ctcss_hz, r.burst_hz, r.type, r.is_linked,
    46→      r.bundesland_code, bl.name as bundesland_name
    47→    FROM exercise_repeaters er
    48→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = er.repeater_id
    49→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = r.bundesland_code
    50→    WHERE er.exercise_id = ?
    51→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, r.sort_order
    52→  `).all(req.params.id);
    53→
    54→  const reports = db.prepare(`
    55→    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.qth as operator_qth, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
    56→      r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
    57→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
    58→    FROM signal_reports sr
    59→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    60→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    61→    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
    62→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?
    63→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, o.callsign
    64→  `).all(req.params.id);
    65→
    66→  const attendance = db.prepare(`
    67→    SELECT ea.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code
    68→    FROM exercise_attendance ea
    69→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = ea.operator_id
    70→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ?
    71→    ORDER BY o.callsign
    72→  `).all(req.params.id);
    73→
    74→  res.json({ ...exercise, repeaters, reports, attendance });
    75→});
    76→
    77→// Update exercise notes/name (admin only)
    78→exercisesRouter.patch('/:id', requireRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
    79→  const db = getDb();
    80→  const { notes, name, oe_link_enabled, organisator, exercise_type, simplex_bezirke, simplex_bl_codes } = req.body;
    81→  const updates: string[] = [];
    82→  const params: any[] = [];
    83→  if (notes !== undefined) { updates.push('notes = ?'); params.push(notes); }
    84→  if (name !== undefined) { updates.push('name = ?'); params.push(name); }
    85→  if (oe_link_enabled !== undefined) { updates.push('oe_link_enabled = ?'); params.push(oe_link_enabled ? 1 : 0); }
    86→  if (organisator !== undefined) { updates.push('organisator = ?'); params.push(organisator || null); }
    87→  if (exercise_type !== undefined) { updates.push('exercise_type = ?'); params.push(exercise_type); }
    88→  if (simplex_bezirke !== undefined) { updates.push('simplex_bezirke = ?'); params.push(simplex_bezirke ? 1 : 0); }
    89→  if (simplex_bl_codes !== undefined) { updates.push('simplex_bl_codes = ?'); params.push(simplex_bl_codes || null); }
    90→  if (updates.length === 0) {
    91→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
    92→    return;
    93→  }
    94→  params.push(req.params.id);
    95→  db.prepare(`UPDATE exercises SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
    96→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    97→  res.json(exercise);
    98→});
    99→
   100→// Delete exercise (admin only)
   101→exercisesRouter.delete('/:id', requireRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
   102→  const db = getDb();
   103→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, date FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
   104→  if (!exercise) {
   105→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });

   108→  const deleteAll = db.transaction(() => {
   109→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   110→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   111→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_repeaters WHERE exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   112→    db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').run(req.params.id);
   113→  });
   114→  deleteAll();
   115→  res.json({ success: true });
   116→});
   117→
   118→// Get exercise stats
   119→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/stats', (req, res) => {
   120→  const db = getDb();
   121→  const eid = req.params.id;
   122→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   123→  const totalReports = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   124→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`
   125→    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
   126→    FROM signal_reports sr
   127→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   128→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   129→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
   130→    ORDER BY r.sort_order
   131→  `).all(eid);
   132→
   133→  // Bezirk breakdown: participants + reports per bezirk, grouped by bundesland
   134→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(`
   135→    SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
   136→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
   137→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   138→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   139→    FROM signal_reports sr
   140→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
   141→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
   142→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   143→    GROUP BY bz.code
   144→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code
   145→  `).all(eid);
   146→
   147→  // Bundesland breakdown
   148→  const blStats = db.prepare(`
   149→    SELECT
   150→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
   151→      o.bundesland_code,
   152→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   153→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   154→    FROM signal_reports sr
   155→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   156→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   157→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   158→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
   159→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code
   160→  `).all(eid);
   161→
   162→  // Participant list: all operators with their report counts
   163→  const participants = db.prepare(`
   164→    SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   165→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
   166→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count,
   167→      GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix) as suffixes
   168→    FROM signal_reports sr
   169→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   170→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   171→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   172→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
   173→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
   174→  `).all(eid);
   175→
   176→  res.json({ totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants });
   177→});

   267→      `).run(req.params.id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability || null, strength || null, db_over_s9 || null, einstiegspunkt_id || null, is_op_marker ? 1 : 0, notes || null, admin.username, bezirk_code || null, suffix || null);
   268→
   269→      db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO exercise_attendance (exercise_id, operator_id, entered_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
   270→        .run(req.params.id, operator_id, admin.username);

   272→      return result;

   275→    const result = insertReportAndAttendance();
   276→

   284→      LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
   285→      WHERE sr.id = ?
   286→    `).get(result.lastInsertRowid);
   287→
   288→    res.status(201).json(report);

   290→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {
   291→      const existing = db.prepare(`

   293→          r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
   294→          ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name

   299→        WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.operator_id = ? AND sr.repeater_id = ?
   300→      `).get(req.params.id, operator_id, repeater_id);
   301→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Rapport für diesen Operator auf diesem Umsetzer existiert bereits', existing_report: existing });
   302→    } else {
   303→      throw e;
   304→    }
   305→  }
   306→});

    27→export default function BundMode({ exerciseId, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    28→  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);
    29→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    30→  const [linkedRepeaters, setLinkedRepeaters] = useState<any[]>([]);
    31→  const [blRepSelection, setBlRepSelection] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
    32→  const [blOpCallsigns, setBlOpCallsigns] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    33→  const [blHomeRepeaters, setBlHomeRepeaters] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    34→  const [collapsedBl, setCollapsedBl] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    35→  const [addRepBl, setAddRepBl] = useState<string | null>(null);
    36→  const [newRepName, setNewRepName] = useState('');
    37→  const [newRepFreq, setNewRepFreq] = useState('');
    38→  const [newRepCallsign, setNewRepCallsign] = useState('');
    39→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    40→  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
    41→  const [dragReportId, setDragReportId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    42→
    43→  useEffect(() => {
    44→    Promise.all([
    45→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender'),
    46→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke'),
    47→      apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters'),
    48→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`),
    49→    ]).then(([bl, bz, allReps, exReps]) => {
    50→      setBundeslaender(bl);
    51→      setBezirke(bz);
    52→      const linked = allReps.filter((r: any) => r.is_linked);
    53→      setLinkedRepeaters(linked);
    54→      // Initialize per-BL defaults: first linked in that BL, else first overall
    55→      const defaults: Record<string, number> = {};
    56→      const ops: Record<string, string> = {};
    57→      const homeReps: Record<string, string> = {};
    58→      for (const b of bl) {
    59→        const inBl = linked.find((r: any) => r.bundesland_code === b.code);
    60→        const repId = inBl ? inBl.id : (linked[0]?.id ?? 0);
    61→        defaults[b.code] = repId;
    62→        // Load existing OP callsign and home repeater from exercise_repeaters
    63→        const exRep = exReps.find((er: any) => er.repeater_id === repId);
    64→        ops[b.code] = exRep?.operator_callsign || '';
    65→        homeReps[b.code] = exRep?.home_repeater || '';
    66→      }
    67→      setBlRepSelection(defaults);
    68→      setBlOpCallsigns(ops);
    69→      setBlHomeRepeaters(homeReps);
    70→    }).catch(() => {});
    71→
    72→    const socket = getSocket();
    73→    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {
    74→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    75→      setBlRepSelection(sel => {
    76→        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {
    77→          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {
    78→            setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));
    79→          }
    80→        }
    81→        return sel;
    82→      });
    83→    };
    84→    const handleHomeRepeaterUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; home_repeater: string }) => {
    85→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    86→      setBlRepSelection(sel => {
    87→        for (const [blCode, repId] of Object.entries(sel)) {
    88→          if (repId === data.repeater_id) {
    89→            setBlHomeRepeaters(prev => ({ ...prev, [blCode]: data.home_repeater || '' }));
    90→          }
    91→        }
    92→        return sel;
    93→      });
    94→    };
    95→    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    96→    socket.on('home_repeater_updated', handleHomeRepeaterUpdated);
    97→    return () => {
    98→      socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    99→      socket.off('home_repeater_updated', handleHomeRepeaterUpdated);
   100→    };
   101→  }, [exerciseId]);
   102→
   103→  const saveOpCallsign = async (blCode: string, val: string) => {
   104→    const repId = blRepSelection[blCode];
   105→    if (!repId) return;
   106→    try {
   107→      // Ensure exercise_repeater exists, then update
   108→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {
   109→        method: 'POST',

   117→      });
   118→    }
   119→    getSocket().emit('op_callsign_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repId, operator_callsign: val });
   120→  };
   121→
   122→  const saveHomeRepeater = async (blCode: string, val: string) => {
   123→    const repId = blRepSelection[blCode];

   125→    try {
   126→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {

   128→        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repId, home_repeater: val || null }),
   129→      });
   130→    } catch {
   131→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters/${repId}`, {
   132→        method: 'PATCH',
   133→        body: JSON.stringify({ home_repeater: val || null }),
   134→      });
   135→    }
   136→    getSocket().emit('home_repeater_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repId, home_repeater: val });
   137→  };
   138→
   139→  const addRepeater = async (blCode: string) => {
   140→    if (!newRepName && !newRepFreq) return;
   141→    const name = newRepName || `Direkte ${newRepFreq}`;
   142→    const freq = newRepFreq ? parseFloat(newRepFreq) : null;
   143→    try {
   144→      const rep = await apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters', {
   145→        method: 'POST',
   146→        body: JSON.stringify({
   147→          short_name: name,
   148→          site_name: name,
   149→          frequency_mhz: freq,
   150→          callsign: newRepCallsign || null,
   151→          band: freq ? (freq > 400 ? '70cm' : freq > 200 ? '23cm' : '2m') : null,
   152→          type: 'repeater',
   153→          bundesland_code: blCode,
   154→        }),
   155→      });
   156→      setLinkedRepeaters(prev => [...prev, rep]);
   157→      setNewRepName('');
   158→      setNewRepFreq('');
   159→      setNewRepCallsign('');
   160→      setAddRepBl(null);
   161→    } catch {}
   162→  };
   163→
   164→  const toggleBl = (code: string) => {
   165→    setCollapsedBl(prev => {
   166→      const next = new Set(prev);
   167→      if (next.has(code)) next.delete(code); else next.add(code);
   168→      return next;
   169→    });
   170→  };
   171→
   172→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
   173→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
   174→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
   175→    return {
   176→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
   177→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
   178→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
   179→    };
   180→  };
   181→
   182→  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => {
   183→    try {
   184→      if (!repeaterId) return;
   185→      const operator = await getOrCreateOperator(form.callsign, form.operator);
   186→      if (!operator) return;
   187→
   188→      const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);
   189→
   190→      try {
   191→        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
   192→          method: 'POST',
   193→          body: JSON.stringify({
   194→            operator_id: operator.id,
   195→            repeater_id: repeaterId,
   196→            ...parsed,
   197→            notes: form.notes || null,
   198→            bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null,
   199→            suffix: form.suffix || null,
   200→          }),
   201→        });
   202→        onReportCreated(report);
   203→      } catch (err: any) {
   204→        if (err.message?.includes('existiert') && err.data?.existing_report) {
   205→          const existing = err.data.existing_report;
   206→          const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {
   207→            method: 'PATCH',
   208→            body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: form.notes || null, bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null, suffix: form.suffix || null }),
   209→          });
   210→          onReportUpdated(updated);
   211→        }
   212→      }
   213→    } catch {
   214→    }
   215→  };
   216→
   217→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {
   218→    setEditingId(report.id);
   219→    setEditForm({
   220→      callsign: report.callsign,
   221→      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign, name: report.operator_name || null, qth: report.operator_qth || null },
   222→      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',
   223→      notes: report.notes || '',
   224→      opName: report.operator_name || '',
   225→      opQth: report.operator_qth || '',
   226→      bezirkCode: report.bezirk_code || '',
   227→      suffix: report.suffix || '',
   228→    });
   229→  };
   230→
   231→  const handleUpdate = async () => {
   232→    if (!editingId) return;
   233→    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);
   234→    try {
   235→      // Save operator name/qth if changed
   236→      if (editForm.operator?.id) {
   237→        const nameChanged = (editForm.opName || '') !== (editForm.operator.name || '');
   238→        const qthChanged = (editForm.opQth || '') !== (editForm.operator.qth || '');
   239→        if (nameChanged || qthChanged) {
   240→          const patch: any = {};
   241→          if (nameChanged) patch.name = editForm.opName || null;
   242→          if (qthChanged) patch.qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   243→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${editForm.operator.id}`, {
   244→            method: 'PATCH',
   245→            body: JSON.stringify(patch),
   246→          });
   247→        }
   248→      }
   249→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
   250→        method: 'PATCH',
   251→        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: editForm.notes || null, bezirk_code: editForm.bezirkCode || null, suffix: editForm.suffix || null }),
   252→      });
   253→      if (editForm.opName !== undefined) updated.operator_name = editForm.opName || null;
   254→      if (editForm.opQth !== undefined) updated.operator_qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   255→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   256→      setEditingId(null);
   257→    } catch {}
   258→  };
   259→
   260→  const handleMoveToBezirk = async (reportId: number, newBezirkCode: string | null) => {
   261→    try {
   262→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, {
   263→        method: 'PATCH',
   264→        body: JSON.stringify({ bezirk_code: newBezirkCode }),
   265→      });
   266→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   267→    } catch {}
   268→  };
   269→
   270→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   271→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   272→    try {
   273→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   274→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   275→    } catch {}
   276→  };
   277→
   278→  const domesticBlCodes = new Set(bundeslaender.filter(b => !b.is_international).map(b => b.code));
   279→
   280→  return (
   281→    <div className="space-y-2">
   282→      {bundeslaender.filter(bl => !bl.is_international).map(bl => {
   283→        const blBezirke = bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === bl.code);
   284→        const defaultRepId = blRepSelection[bl.code] || 0;
   285→        // Match reports by bezirk's Bundesland, only for linked repeaters
   286→        const blBezirkCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   287→        const blReports = reports.filter(r => {
   288→          if (!r.repeater_is_linked) return false;
   289→          // Primary: match by bezirk code
   290→          if (r.bezirk_code && blBezirkCodes.has(r.bezirk_code)) return true;
   291→          // Fallback for null bezirk_code: use operator BL (if domestic), else repeater BL
   292→          if (!r.bezirk_code) {
   293→            const effectiveBl = domesticBlCodes.has(r.bundesland_code) ? r.bundesland_code : r.repeater_bundesland_code;
   294→            return effectiveBl === bl.code;
   295→          }
   296→          return false;
   297→        });
   298→        const isCollapsed = collapsedBl.has(bl.code);
   299→        const reportCount = blReports.filter(r => !r.is_op_marker && r.readability).length;
   300→
   301→        return (
   302→          <div key={bl.code} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">
   303→            <div className="bg-gray-50 px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b gap-1">
   304→              <div
   305→                onClick={() => toggleBl(bl.code)}
   306→                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 rounded px-1 -mx-1 min-w-0"
   307→              >
   308→                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>
   309→                <span className="font-medium text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">OE{parseInt(bl.code)} {bl.name}</span>
   310→                <span className="bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0">{reportCount}</span>
   311→              </div>
   312→              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
   313→                <button
   314→                  onClick={() => setAddRepBl(addRepBl === bl.code ? null : bl.code)}
   315→                  className="text-xs text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 hidden sm:inline"
   316→                >
   317→                  + Umsetzer
   318→                </button>
   319→                <label className={`text-xs ${blOpCallsigns[bl.code] ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-[#c8102e] font-semibold'}`}>OP:</label>
   320→                <input
   321→                  type="text"
   322→                  value={blOpCallsigns[bl.code] || ''}
   323→                  onChange={e => setBlOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [bl.code]: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   324→                  onBlur={e => saveOpCallsign(bl.code, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   325→                  placeholder="Rufz."
   326→                  className={`border rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 ${blOpCallsigns[bl.code] ? 'border-gray-300' : 'border-[#c8102e] placeholder-[#c8102e]/60'}`}
   327→                />
   328→                <label className="text-xs text-gray-500 hidden sm:inline">REP:</label>
   329→                <select
   330→                  value={blHomeRepeaters[bl.code] || ''}
   331→                  onChange={e => {
   332→                    const val = e.target.value;
   333→                    setBlHomeRepeaters(prev => ({ ...prev, [bl.code]: val }));
   334→                    saveHomeRepeater(bl.code, val);
   335→                  }}
   336→                  className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24 sm:w-32 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   337→                >
   338→                  <option value="">—</option>
   339→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code === bl.code).map(r => (
   340→                    <option key={r.id} value={r.short_name}>{r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.band ? ` ${r.band}` : ''}</option>
   341→                  ))}
   342→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code !== bl.code).length > 0 && (
   343→                    <option disabled>──────</option>
   344→                  )}
   345→                  {linkedRepeaters.filter(r => r.bundesland_code !== bl.code).map(r => (
   346→                    <option key={r.id} value={r.short_name}>{r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.band ? ` ${r.band}` : ''}</option>
   347→                  ))}
   348→                </select>
   349→              </div>
   350→            </div>
   351→
   352→            {!isCollapsed && (
   353→              <div>
   354→                {addRepBl === bl.code && (
   355→                  <div className="px-3 py-2 bg-blue-50 border-b flex items-center gap-1 flex-wrap">
   356→                    <input
   357→                      type="text"
   358→                      value={newRepName}
   359→                      onChange={e => setNewRepName(e.target.value)}
   360→                      placeholder="Name"
   361→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-28 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   362→                      autoFocus
   363→                    />
   364→                    <input
   365→                      type="text"
   366→                      value={newRepFreq}
   367→                      onChange={e => setNewRepFreq(e.target.value)}
   368→                      placeholder="MHz"
   369→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   370→                    />
   371→                    <input
   372→                      type="text"
   373→                      value={newRepCallsign}
   374→                      onChange={e => setNewRepCallsign(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   375→                      placeholder="Rufz."
   376→                      className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   377→                    />
   378→                    <button
   379→                      onClick={() => addRepeater(bl.code)}
   380→                      className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs"
   381→                    >
   382→                      OK
   383→                    </button>
   384→                    <button
   385→                      onClick={() => { setAddRepBl(null); setNewRepName(''); setNewRepFreq(''); setNewRepCallsign(''); }}
   386→                      className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 text-xs"
   387→                    >
   388→                      Abbrechen
   389→                    </button>
   390→                  </div>
   391→                )}
   392→                <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   393→                  {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   394→                    const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   395→                    return (
   396→                      <BezirkRow
   397→                        key={bz.code}
   398→                        bezirk={bz}
   399→                        reports={bzReports}
   400→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   401→                        allBezirke={bezirke}
   402→                        allBundeslaender={bundeslaender}
   403→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   404→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   405→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, repeaterId, form)}
   406→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   407→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   408→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   409→                        editingId={editingId}
   410→                        editForm={editForm}
   411→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   412→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   413→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   414→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   415→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   416→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   417→                      />
   418→                    );
   419→                  })}
   420→                  {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk + entry form */}
   421→                  {(() => {
   422→                    const bzCodes = new Set(blBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   423→                    const unassigned = blReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   424→                    return (
   425→                      <BezirkRow
   426→                        bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   427→                        reports={unassigned}
   428→                        availableBezirke={blBezirke}
   429→                        allBezirke={bezirke}
   430→                        allBundeslaender={bundeslaender}
   431→                        linkedRepeaters={linkedRepeaters}
   432→                        defaultRepeaterId={defaultRepId}
   433→                        onSubmit={(repeaterId, form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', repeaterId, form)}
   434→                        onEdit={handleEdit}
   435→                        onDelete={handleDelete}
   436→                        onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   437→                        editingId={editingId}
   438→                        editForm={editForm}
   439→                        onEditChange={setEditForm}
   440→                        onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   441→                        onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   442→                        dragReportId={dragReportId}
   443→                        onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   444→                        onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   445→                      />
   446→                    );
   447→                  })()}
   448→                </div>
   449→              </div>
   450→            )}
   451→          </div>
   452→        );
   453→      })}
   454→    </div>
   455→  );
   456→}
   457→
   458→interface BezirkRowProps {
   459→  bezirk: any;
   460→  reports: any[];
   461→  availableBezirke: any[];
   462→  allBezirke: any[];
   463→  allBundeslaender: any[];
   464→  linkedRepeaters: any[];
   465→  defaultRepeaterId: number;
   466→  onSubmit: (repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => void;
   467→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   468→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   469→  onMoveToBezirk: (reportId: number, bezirkCode: string | null) => void;
   470→  editingId: number | null;
   471→  editForm: EntryForm;
   472→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   473→  onEditSave: () => void;
   474→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   475→  dragReportId: number | null;
   476→  onDragStart: (reportId: number) => void;
   477→  onDragEnd: () => void;
   478→}
   479→
   480→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, availableBezirke, allBezirke, allBundeslaender, linkedRepeaters, defaultRepeaterId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, onMoveToBezirk, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel, dragReportId, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: BezirkRowProps) {
   481→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   482→  const [repeaterId, setRepeaterId] = useState(defaultRepeaterId);
   483→  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
   484→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   485→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   486→
   487→  useEffect(() => {
   488→    if (defaultRepeaterId && !repeaterId) setRepeaterId(defaultRepeaterId);
   489→  }, [defaultRepeaterId]);
   490→
   491→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   492→    e.preventDefault();
   493→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   494→    if (!repeaterId) return;
   495→    onSubmit(repeaterId, form);
   496→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   497→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   498→  };
   499→
   500→  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   501→    e.preventDefault();
   502→    setDragOver(false);
   503→    if (dragReportId) {
   504→      const targetCode = bezirk.code === '??' ? null : bezirk.code;
   505→      onMoveToBezirk(dragReportId, targetCode);
   506→    }
   507→  };
   508→
   509→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   510→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   511→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   512→    if (value === original) return;
   513→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   514→      method: 'PATCH',
   515→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   516→    }).then(() => {
   517→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   518→    }).catch(() => {});
   519→  };
   520→
   521→  return (
   522→    <div
   523→      className={`px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors ${dragOver && dragReportId ? 'bg-blue-100 ring-2 ring-blue-400 ring-inset' : ''}`}
   524→      onDragOver={e => { if (dragReportId) { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); } }}
   525→      onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
   526→      onDrop={handleDrop}
   527→    >
   528→      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-1">
   529→        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.code === '??' ? 'bg-[#ffc107] text-gray-800' : bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   530→          {bezirk.code === '??' ? '??' : bezirk.code}
   531→        </span>
   532→        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{bezirk.name}</span>
   533→        {reports.length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0">({reports.length})</span>}
   534→      </div>
   535→
   536→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   537→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   538→          <tbody>
   539→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   540→              <tr
   541→                key={r.id}
   542→                className={`hover:bg-blue-50 group ${dragReportId === r.id ? 'opacity-40' : ''}`}
   543→                draggable={editingId !== r.id}
   544→                onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; onDragStart(r.id); }}
   545→                onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
   546→              >
   547→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   548→                  <td colSpan={6} className="py-0.5">
   549→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   550→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   551→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   552→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   553→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   554→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   555→                      <select
   556→                        value={editForm.bezirkCode}
   557→                        onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, bezirkCode: e.target.value })}
   558→                        className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24"
   559→                        title="Bezirk"
   560→                      >
   561→                        <option value="">??</option>
   562→                        {allBundeslaender.filter(b => !b.is_international).map(b => {
   563→                          const blBz = allBezirke.filter(bz => bz.bundesland_code === b.code);
   564→                          if (blBz.length === 0) return null;
   565→                          return (
   566→                            <optgroup key={b.code} label={`OE${parseInt(b.code)} ${b.name}`}>
   567→                              {blBz.map(bz => <option key={bz.code} value={bz.code}>{bz.code} {bz.name}</option>)}
   568→                            </optgroup>
   569→                          );
   570→                        })}
   571→                      </select>
   572→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   573→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   574→                    </div>
   575→                  </td>
   576→                ) : (
   577→                  <>
   578→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4 cursor-grab">{idx + 1}</td>
   579→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   580→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}{r.suffix || ''}</span>
   581→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   582→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   583→                    </td>
   584→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-blue-600 text-xs cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.repeater_name || ''}</td>
   585→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   586→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   587→                    </td>
   588→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   589→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   590→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   591→                    </td>
   592→                  </>
   593→                )}
   594→              </tr>
   595→            ))}
   596→          </tbody>
   597→        </table>
   598→      )}
   599→
   600→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   601→        {linkedRepeaters.length > 0 && (
   602→          <select
   603→            value={repeaterId || ''}
   604→            onChange={e => setRepeaterId(parseInt(e.target.value))}
   605→            className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs max-w-[80px] sm:max-w-none focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   606→          >
   607→            {linkedRepeaters.map(r => (
   608→              <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
   609→                {r.site_name || r.short_name}{r.callsign ? ` (${r.callsign})` : ''}
   610→              </option>
   611→            ))}
   612→          </select>
   613→        )}
   614→        <CallsignInput
   615→          ref={callsignRef}
   616→          value={form.callsign}
   617→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   618→          onSelect={op => {
   619→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   620→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   621→          }}
   622→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   623→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   624→        />
   625→        <div className="flex gap-0.5">
   626→          {SUFFIXES.map(s => (
   627→            <button
   628→              key={s}
   629→              type="button"
   630→              onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, suffix: f.suffix === s ? '' : s }))}
   631→              className={`px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono border ${form.suffix === s ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}
   632→              title={s}
   633→            >
   634→              {s.slice(1)}
   635→            </button>
   636→          ))}
   637→        </div>
   638→        <input
   639→          type="text"
   640→          value={form.opName}
   641→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   642→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   643→          placeholder="Name"
   644→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   645→        />
   646→        <input
   647→          type="text"
   648→          value={form.opQth}
   649→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   650→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   651→          placeholder="QTH"
   652→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   653→        />
   654→        <input
   655→          ref={rapportRef}
   656→          type="text"
   657→          value={form.rapport}
   658→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   659→          placeholder="5/9"
   660→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   661→          onKeyDown={e => {
   662→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   663→              e.preventDefault();
   664→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   665→            }
   666→          }}
   667→        />
   668→        <input
   669→          type="text"
   670→          value={form.notes}
   671→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   672→          placeholder="Sonst."
   673→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   674→        />
   675→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   676→          + ↵
   677→        </button>
   678→      </form>
   679→    </div>
   680→  );
   681→}
