     1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef, type FormEvent } from 'react';
     2→import { apiFetch, getOrCreateOperator } from '../services/api';
     3→import { getSocket } from '../services/socket';
     4→import CallsignInput, { type CallsignInputRef } from './CallsignInput';
     5→
     6→const BUNDESLAND_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
     7→  '01': 'OE1 Wien',
     8→  '02': 'OE2 Salzburg',
     9→  '03': 'OE3 Niederösterreich',
    10→  '04': 'OE4 Burgenland',
    11→  '05': 'OE5 Oberösterreich',
    12→  '06': 'OE6 Steiermark',
    13→  '07': 'OE7 Tirol',
    14→  '08': 'OE8 Kärnten',
    15→  '09': 'OE9 Vorarlberg',
    16→  '10': 'Slowenien',
    17→};
    18→
    19→interface Props {
    20→  exerciseId: string;
    21→  repeaters: any[];
    22→  reports: any[];
    23→  simplexBezirke?: boolean;
    24→  simplexBlCodes?: string;
    25→  onReportCreated: (report: any) => void;
    26→  onReportUpdated: (report: any) => void;
    27→  onReportDeleted: (reportId: number) => void;
    28→}
    29→
    30→interface EntryForm {
    31→  callsign: string;
    32→  operator: any;
    33→  rapport: string;
    34→  notes: string;
    35→  opName: string;
    36→  opQth: string;
    37→  bezirkCode: string;
    38→  suffix?: string;
    39→}
    40→
    41→const SUFFIXES = ['/m', '/p', '/am', '/mm'] as const;
    42→
    43→export default function LandMode({ exerciseId, repeaters, reports, simplexBezirke, simplexBlCodes, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    44→  const [collapsedReps, setCollapsedReps] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
    45→  const [opCallsigns, setOpCallsigns] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
    46→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    47→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    48→  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
    49→  const [dragReportId, setDragReportId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    50→
    51→  // Load bezirke + OP callsigns
    52→  useEffect(() => {
    53→    Promise.all([
    54→      apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke'),
    55→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`),
    56→    ]).then(([bz, exReps]) => {
    57→      setBezirke(bz);
    58→      const ops: Record<number, string> = {};
    59→      for (const er of exReps) {
    60→        if (er.operator_callsign) {
    61→          ops[er.repeater_id] = er.operator_callsign;
    62→        }
    63→      }
    64→      setOpCallsigns(ops);
    65→    }).catch(() => {});
    66→
    67→    const socket = getSocket();
    68→    const handleOpCallsignUpdated = (data: { exercise_id: string; repeater_id: number; operator_callsign: string }) => {
    69→      if (data.exercise_id !== exerciseId) return;
    70→      setOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [data.repeater_id]: data.operator_callsign || '' }));
    71→    };
    72→    socket.on('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated);
    73→    return () => { socket.off('op_callsign_updated', handleOpCallsignUpdated); };
    74→  }, [exerciseId]);
    75→
    76→  // Group repeaters by Bundesland — exclude linked repeaters (those belong to BundMode)
    77→  const repeatersByBl: Record<string, any[]> = {};
    78→  const simplexRepeaters: any[] = [];
    79→  for (const r of repeaters) {
    80→    if (r.is_linked) continue;
    81→    if (r.type === 'simplex') {
    82→      simplexRepeaters.push(r);
    83→    } else {
    84→      const blCode = r.bundesland_code || '_other';
    85→      if (!repeatersByBl[blCode]) repeatersByBl[blCode] = [];
    86→      repeatersByBl[blCode].push(r);
    87→    }
    88→  }
    89→
    90→  const activeBlCodes = simplexBlCodes ? simplexBlCodes.split(',').filter(Boolean) : ['01', '02', '03', '04', '05', '06', '07', '08', '09'];
    91→
    92→  const sortedBlCodes = Object.keys(repeatersByBl).sort((a, b) => {
    93→    if (a === '_other') return 1;
    94→    if (b === '_other') return -1;
    95→    return a.localeCompare(b);
    96→  });
    97→
    98→  const toggleRepeater = (repId: number) => {
    99→    setCollapsedReps(prev => {
   100→      const next = new Set(prev);
   101→      if (next.has(repId)) next.delete(repId); else next.add(repId);
   102→      return next;
   103→    });
   104→  };
   105→
   106→  const saveOpCallsign = async (repeaterId: number, val: string) => {
   107→    try {
   108→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters`, {
   109→        method: 'POST',
   110→        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repeaterId, operator_callsign: val || null }),
   111→      });
   112→    } catch {
   113→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/repeaters/${repeaterId}`, {
   114→        method: 'PATCH',
   115→        body: JSON.stringify({ operator_callsign: val || null }),
   116→      });
   117→    }
   118→    getSocket().emit('op_callsign_updated', { exercise_id: exerciseId, repeater_id: repeaterId, operator_callsign: val });
   119→  };
   120→
   121→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
   122→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
   123→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
   124→    return {
   125→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
   126→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
   127→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
   128→    };
   129→  };
   130→
   131→  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, repeaterId: number, form: EntryForm) => {
   132→    try {
   133→      const operator = await getOrCreateOperator(form.callsign, form.operator);
   134→      if (!operator) return;
   135→
   136→      const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);
   137→
   138→      try {
   139→        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
   140→          method: 'POST',
   141→          body: JSON.stringify({
   142→            operator_id: operator.id,
   143→            repeater_id: repeaterId,
   144→            ...parsed,
   145→            notes: form.notes || null,
   146→            bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null,
   147→            suffix: form.suffix || null,
   148→          }),
   149→        });
   150→        onReportCreated(report);
   151→      } catch (err: any) {
   152→        if (err.message?.includes('existiert') && err.data?.existing_report) {
   153→          const existing = err.data.existing_report;
   154→          const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {
   155→            method: 'PATCH',
   156→            body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: form.notes || null, suffix: form.suffix || null }),
   157→          });
   158→          onReportUpdated(updated);
   159→        }
   160→      }
   161→    } catch {}
   162→  };
   163→
   164→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {
   165→    setEditingId(report.id);
   166→    setEditForm({
   167→      callsign: report.callsign,
   168→      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign, name: report.operator_name || null, qth: report.operator_qth || null },
   169→      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',
   170→      notes: report.notes || '',
   171→      opName: report.operator_name || '',
   172→      opQth: report.operator_qth || '',
   173→      bezirkCode: report.bezirk_code || '',
   174→      suffix: report.suffix || '',
   175→    });
   176→  };
   177→
   178→  const handleUpdate = async () => {
   179→    if (!editingId) return;
   180→    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);
   181→    try {
   182→      // Save operator name/qth if changed
   183→      if (editForm.operator?.id) {
   184→        const nameChanged = (editForm.opName || '') !== (editForm.operator.name || '');
   185→        const qthChanged = (editForm.opQth || '') !== (editForm.operator.qth || '');
   186→        if (nameChanged || qthChanged) {
   187→          const patch: any = {};
   188→          if (nameChanged) patch.name = editForm.opName || null;
   189→          if (qthChanged) patch.qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   190→          await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${editForm.operator.id}`, {
   191→            method: 'PATCH',
   192→            body: JSON.stringify(patch),
   193→          });
   194→        }
   195→      }
   196→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
   197→        method: 'PATCH',
   198→        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: editForm.notes || null, bezirk_code: editForm.bezirkCode || null, suffix: editForm.suffix || null }),
   199→      });
   200→      // Update the returned report with new name/qth so display refreshes
   201→      if (editForm.opName !== undefined) updated.operator_name = editForm.opName || null;
   202→      if (editForm.opQth !== undefined) updated.operator_qth = editForm.opQth || null;
   203→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   204→      setEditingId(null);
   205→    } catch {}
   206→  };
   207→
   208→  const handleMoveToBezirk = async (reportId: number, newBezirkCode: string | null) => {
   209→    try {
   210→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, {
   211→        method: 'PATCH',
   212→        body: JSON.stringify({ bezirk_code: newBezirkCode }),
   213→      });
   214→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   215→    } catch {}
   216→  };
   217→
   218→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   219→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   220→    try {
   221→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   222→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   223→    } catch {}
   224→  };
   225→
   226→  const renderRepeaterCard = (rep: any) => {
   227→    const repReports = reports.filter(r => r.repeater_id === rep.repeater_id && !r.is_op_marker);
   228→    const reportCount = repReports.filter(r => r.readability).length;
   229→    const isCollapsed = collapsedReps.has(rep.repeater_id);
   230→    const isSimplexGrouped = simplexBezirke && rep.type === 'simplex';
   231→    const repBezirke = isSimplexGrouped
   232→      ? bezirke.filter(b => activeBlCodes.includes(b.bundesland_code))
   233→      : bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === rep.bundesland_code);
   234→
   235→    // Group bezirke by BL for simplex grouped rendering
   236→    const bezirkeByBl: Record<string, any[]> = {};
   237→    if (isSimplexGrouped) {
   238→      for (const bz of repBezirke) {
   239→        if (!bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code]) bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code] = [];
   240→        bezirkeByBl[bz.bundesland_code].push(bz);
   241→      }
   242→    }
   243→
   244→    return (
   245→      <div key={rep.repeater_id} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm">
   246→        <div className="bg-gray-50 px-2 sm:px-3 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between border-b gap-1">
   247→          <div
   248→            onClick={() => toggleRepeater(rep.repeater_id)}
   249→            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 rounded px-1 -mx-1 min-w-0"
   250→          >
   251→            <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>
   252→            <span className="font-medium text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">{rep.short_name}{rep.repeater_callsign ? ` (${rep.repeater_callsign})` : ''}</span>
   253→            <span className="bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0">{reportCount}</span>
   254→            {rep.frequency_mhz && (
   255→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden sm:inline">{rep.frequency_mhz} MHz</span>
   256→            )}
   257→            {rep.offset_mhz && (
   258→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden md:inline">({rep.offset_mhz > 0 ? '+' : ''}{rep.offset_mhz})</span>
   259→            )}
   260→            {rep.ctcss_hz && (
   261→              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 hidden md:inline">CTCSS {rep.ctcss_hz}</span>
   262→            )}
   263→          </div>
   264→          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
   265→            <label className={`text-xs ${opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] ? 'text-gray-500' : 'text-[#c8102e] font-semibold'}`}>OP:</label>
   266→            <input
   267→              type="text"
   268→              value={opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] || ''}
   269→              onChange={e => setOpCallsigns(prev => ({ ...prev, [rep.repeater_id]: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   270→              onBlur={e => saveOpCallsign(rep.repeater_id, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   271→              placeholder="Rufz."
   272→              className={`border rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-mono uppercase w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 ${opCallsigns[rep.repeater_id] ? 'border-gray-300' : 'border-[#c8102e] placeholder-[#c8102e]/60'}`}
   273→            />
   274→          </div>
   275→        </div>
   276→
   277→        {!isCollapsed && (
   278→          <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   279→            {repBezirke.length > 0 ? (
   280→              <>
   281→                {isSimplexGrouped ? (
   282→                  /* Simplex grouped by BL → Bezirk */
   283→                  <>
   284→                    {activeBlCodes.sort().map(blCode => {
   285→                      const blBezirke = bezirkeByBl[blCode] || [];
   286→                      if (blBezirke.length === 0) return null;
   287→                      const blLabel = BUNDESLAND_NAMES[blCode] || blCode;
   288→                      return (
   289→                        <div key={blCode}>
   290→                          <div className="bg-gray-100 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1">
   291→                            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide">{blLabel}</span>
   292→                          </div>
   293→                          {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   294→                            const bzReports = repReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   295→                            return (
   296→                              <BezirkRow
   297→                                key={bz.code}
   298→                                bezirk={bz}
   299→                                reports={bzReports}
   300→                                repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   301→                                availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   302→                                onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, rep.repeater_id, form)}
   303→                                onEdit={handleEdit}
   304→                                onDelete={handleDelete}
   305→                                onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   306→                                editingId={editingId}
   307→                                editForm={editForm}
   308→                                onEditChange={setEditForm}
   309→                                onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   310→                                onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   311→                                dragReportId={dragReportId}
   312→                                onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   313→                                onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   314→                              />
   315→                            );
   316→                          })}
   317→                        </div>
   318→                      );
   319→                    })}
   320→                    {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk */}
   321→                    {(() => {
   322→                      const bzCodes = new Set(repBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   323→                      const unassigned = repReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   324→                      return (
   325→                        <BezirkRow
   326→                          bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   327→                          reports={unassigned}
   328→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   329→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   330→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   331→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   332→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   333→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   334→                          editingId={editingId}
   335→                          editForm={editForm}
   336→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   337→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   338→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   339→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   340→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   341→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   342→                        />
   343→                      );
   344→                    })()}
   345→                  </>
   346→                ) : (
   347→                  /* Normal repeater with bezirke */
   348→                  <>
   349→                    {repBezirke.map(bz => {
   350→                      const bzReports = repReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   351→                      return (
   352→                        <BezirkRow
   353→                          key={bz.code}
   354→                          bezirk={bz}
   355→                          reports={bzReports}
   356→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   357→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   358→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, rep.repeater_id, form)}
   359→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   360→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   361→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   362→                          editingId={editingId}
   363→                          editForm={editForm}
   364→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   365→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   366→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   367→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   368→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   369→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   370→                        />
   371→                      );
   372→                    })}
   373→                    {/* Sonstige — reports without matching bezirk + entry form */}
   374→                    {(() => {
   375→                      const bzCodes = new Set(repBezirke.map(b => b.code));
   376→                      const unassigned = repReports.filter(r => !r.bezirk_code || !bzCodes.has(r.bezirk_code));
   377→                      return (
   378→                        <BezirkRow
   379→                          bezirk={{ code: '??', name: 'Sonstige', is_capital: false }}
   380→                          reports={unassigned}
   381→                          repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   382→                          availableBezirke={repBezirke}
   383→                          onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('??', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   384→                          onEdit={handleEdit}
   385→                          onDelete={handleDelete}
   386→                          onMoveToBezirk={handleMoveToBezirk}
   387→                          editingId={editingId}
   388→                          editForm={editForm}
   389→                          onEditChange={setEditForm}
   390→                          onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   391→                          onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   392→                          dragReportId={dragReportId}
   393→                          onDragStart={setDragReportId}
   394→                          onDragEnd={() => setDragReportId(null)}
   395→                        />
   396→                      );
   397→                    })()}
   398→                  </>
   399→                )}
   400→              </>
   401→            ) : (
   402→              /* No Bezirke — flat list with input form */
   403→              <FlatReportRow
   404→                reports={repReports}
   405→                repeaterId={rep.repeater_id}
   406→                onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit('', rep.repeater_id, form)}
   407→                onEdit={handleEdit}
   408→                onDelete={handleDelete}
   409→                editingId={editingId}
   410→                editForm={editForm}
   411→                onEditChange={setEditForm}
   412→                onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   413→                onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   414→              />
   415→            )}
   416→          </div>
   417→        )}
   418→      </div>
   419→    );
   420→  };
   421→
   422→  return (
   423→    <div className="space-y-2">
   424→      {sortedBlCodes.map(blCode => {
   425→        const blReps = repeatersByBl[blCode];
   426→        const label = BUNDESLAND_NAMES[blCode] || 'Sonstige';
   427→        return (
   428→          <div key={blCode}>
   429→            <div className="px-1 mb-1 mt-2">
   430→              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">{label}</span>
   431→            </div>
   432→            <div className="space-y-2">
   433→              {blReps.map(rep => renderRepeaterCard(rep))}
   434→            </div>
   435→          </div>
   436→        );
   437→      })}
   438→
   439→      {simplexRepeaters.length > 0 && (
   440→        <div>
   441→          <div className="px-1 mb-1 mt-2">
   442→            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Simplex</span>
   443→          </div>
   444→          <div className="space-y-2">
   445→            {simplexRepeaters.map(rep => renderRepeaterCard(rep))}
   446→          </div>
   447→        </div>
   448→      )}
   449→    </div>
   450→  );
   451→}
   452→
   453→interface BezirkRowProps {
   454→  bezirk: any;
   455→  reports: any[];
   456→  repeaterId: number;
   457→  availableBezirke: any[];
   458→  onSubmit: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   459→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   460→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   461→  onMoveToBezirk: (reportId: number, bezirkCode: string | null) => void;
   462→  editingId: number | null;
   463→  editForm: EntryForm;
   464→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   465→  onEditSave: () => void;
   466→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   467→  dragReportId: number | null;
   468→  onDragStart: (reportId: number) => void;
   469→  onDragEnd: () => void;
   470→}
   471→
   472→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, repeaterId, availableBezirke, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, onMoveToBezirk, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel, dragReportId, onDragStart, onDragEnd }: BezirkRowProps) {
   473→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   474→  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);
   475→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   476→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   477→
   478→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   479→    e.preventDefault();
   480→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   481→    onSubmit(form);
   482→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '', suffix: '' });
   483→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   484→  };
   485→
   486→  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   487→    e.preventDefault();
   488→    setDragOver(false);
   489→    if (dragReportId) {
   490→      const targetCode = bezirk.code === '??' ? null : bezirk.code;
   491→      onMoveToBezirk(dragReportId, targetCode);
   492→    }
   493→  };
   494→
   495→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   496→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   497→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   498→    if (value === original) return;
   499→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   500→      method: 'PATCH',
   501→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   502→    }).then(() => {
   503→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   504→    }).catch(() => {});
   505→  };
   506→
   507→  return (
   508→    <div
   509→      className={`px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 transition-colors ${dragOver && dragReportId ? 'bg-blue-100 ring-2 ring-blue-400 ring-inset' : ''}`}
   510→      onDragOver={e => { if (dragReportId) { e.preventDefault(); setDragOver(true); } }}
   511→      onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}
   512→      onDrop={handleDrop}
   513→    >
   514→      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 mb-1">
   515→        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.code === '??' ? 'bg-[#ffc107] text-gray-800' : bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   516→          {bezirk.code === '??' ? '??' : bezirk.code}
   517→        </span>
   518→        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate">{bezirk.name}</span>
   519→        {reports.length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 flex-shrink-0">({reports.length})</span>}
   520→      </div>
   521→
   522→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   523→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   524→          <tbody>
   525→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   526→              <tr
   527→                key={r.id}
   528→                className={`hover:bg-blue-50 group ${dragReportId === r.id ? 'opacity-40' : ''}`}
   529→                draggable={editingId !== r.id}
   530→                onDragStart={e => { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; onDragStart(r.id); }}
   531→                onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
   532→              >
   533→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   534→                  <td colSpan={5} className="py-0.5">
   535→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   536→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   537→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   538→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   539→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   540→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   541→                      <select
   542→                        value={editForm.bezirkCode}
   543→                        onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, bezirkCode: e.target.value })}
   544→                        className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16"
   545→                        title="Bezirk"
   546→                      >
   547→                        <option value="">??</option>
   548→                        {availableBezirke.map(bz => <option key={bz.code} value={bz.code}>{bz.code}</option>)}
   549→                      </select>
   550→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   551→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   552→                    </div>
   553→                  </td>
   554→                ) : (
   555→                  <>
   556→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4 cursor-grab">{idx + 1}</td>
   557→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   558→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}{r.suffix || ''}</span>
   559→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   560→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   561→                    </td>
   562→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   563→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   564→                    </td>
   565→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   566→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   567→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   568→                    </td>
   569→                  </>
   570→                )}
   571→              </tr>
   572→            ))}
   573→          </tbody>
   574→        </table>
   575→      )}
   576→
   577→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   578→        <CallsignInput
   579→          ref={callsignRef}
   580→          value={form.callsign}
   581→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   582→          onSelect={op => {
   583→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   584→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   585→          }}
   586→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   587→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   588→        />
   589→        <div className="flex gap-0.5">
   590→          {SUFFIXES.map(s => (
   591→            <button
   592→              key={s}
   593→              type="button"
   594→              onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, suffix: f.suffix === s ? '' : s }))}
   595→              className={`px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono border ${form.suffix === s ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}
   596→              title={s}
   597→            >
   598→              {s.slice(1)}
   599→            </button>
   600→          ))}
   601→        </div>
   602→        <input
   603→          type="text"
   604→          value={form.opName}
   605→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opName: e.target.value }))}
   606→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('name', e.target.value)}
   607→          placeholder="Name"
   608→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-20 sm:w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   609→        />
   610→        <input
   611→          type="text"
   612→          value={form.opQth}
   613→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, opQth: e.target.value }))}
   614→          onBlur={e => saveOperatorField('qth', e.target.value)}
   615→          placeholder="QTH"
   616→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 hidden sm:block"
   617→        />
   618→        <input
   619→          ref={rapportRef}
   620→          type="text"
   621→          value={form.rapport}
   622→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   623→          placeholder="5/9"
   624→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   625→          onKeyDown={e => {
   626→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   627→              e.preventDefault();
   628→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   629→            }
   630→          }}
   631→        />
   632→        <input
   633→          type="text"
   634→          value={form.notes}
   635→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}
   636→          placeholder="Sonst."
   637→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[40px] sm:min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
   638→        />
   639→        <button type="submit" title="Rapport speichern (Enter)" className="bg-[#c8102e] hover:bg-[#a00d24] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs flex-shrink-0">
   640→          + ↵
   641→        </button>
   642→      </form>
   643→    </div>
   644→  );
   645→}
   646→
   647→interface FlatReportRowProps {
   648→  reports: any[];
   649→  repeaterId: number;
   650→  onSubmit: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   651→  onEdit: (report: any) => void;
   652→  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
   653→  editingId: number | null;
   654→  editForm: EntryForm;
   655→  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;
   656→  onEditSave: () => void;
   657→  onEditCancel: () => void;
   658→}
   659→
   660→function FlatReportRow({ reports, repeaterId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel }: FlatReportRowProps) {
   661→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   662→  const callsignRef = useRef<CallsignInputRef>(null);
   663→  const rapportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   664→
   665→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   666→    e.preventDefault();
   667→    if (!form.callsign && !form.operator) return;
   668→    onSubmit(form);
   669→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '5/9', notes: '', opName: '', opQth: '', bezirkCode: '' });
   670→    setTimeout(() => callsignRef.current?.focus(), 50);
   671→  };
   672→
   673→  const saveOperatorField = (field: 'name' | 'qth', value: string) => {
   674→    if (!form.operator?.id) return;
   675→    const original = field === 'name' ? (form.operator.name || '') : (form.operator.qth || '');
   676→    if (value === original) return;
   677→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${form.operator.id}`, {
   678→      method: 'PATCH',
   679→      body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value || null }),
   680→    }).then(() => {
   681→      setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: { ...f.operator, [field]: value || null } }));
   682→    }).catch(() => {});
   683→  };
   684→
   685→  return (
   686→    <div className="px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5">
   687→      {reports.length > 0 && (
   688→        <table className="w-full text-xs mb-1">
   689→          <tbody>
   690→            {reports.map((r, idx) => (
   691→              <tr key={r.id} className="hover:bg-blue-50 group">
   692→                {editingId === r.id ? (
   693→                  <td colSpan={5} className="py-0.5">
   694→                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 rounded p-1 flex-wrap" onKeyDown={e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); onEditSave(); } }}>
   695→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   696→                      <input value={editForm.opName} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20 hidden sm:block" />
   697→                      <input value={editForm.opQth} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, opQth: e.target.value })} placeholder="QTH" className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-14 sm:w-16 hidden sm:block" />
   698→                      <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" autoFocus />
   699→                      <input value={editForm.notes} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, notes: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sonst." className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-16 sm:w-20" />
   700→                      <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   701→                      <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   702→                    </div>
   703→                  </td>
   704→                ) : (
   705→                  <>
   706→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-400 w-4">{idx + 1}</td>
   707→                    <td className="py-0.5 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   708→                      <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}{r.suffix || ''}</span>
   709→                      {r.operator_name && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs">{r.operator_name}</span>}
   710→                      {r.operator_qth && <span className="ml-1 text-gray-400 text-xs hidden sm:inline">{r.operator_qth}</span>}
   711→                    </td>
   712→                    <td className="py-0.5 font-mono text-gray-500 w-16 sm:w-24 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>
   713→                      {r.readability && r.strength ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '—'}
   714→                    </td>
   715→                    <td className="py-0.5 text-gray-500 cursor-pointer hidden sm:table-cell" onClick={() => onEdit(r)}>{r.notes || ''}</td>
   716→                    <td className="py-0.5 w-4">
   717→                      <button onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">x</button>
   718→                    </td>
   719→                  </>
   720→                )}
   721→              </tr>
   722→            ))}
   723→          </tbody>
   724→        </table>
   725→      )}
   726→
   727→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   728→        <CallsignInput
   729→          ref={callsignRef}
   730→          value={form.callsign}
   731→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v, operator: null, opName: '', opQth: '' }))}
   732→          onSelect={op => {
   733→            setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign, opName: op.name || '', opQth: op.qth || '' }));
   734→            setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50);
   735→          }}
   736→          onEnter={() => setTimeout(() => { const el = rapportRef.current; if (el) { el.focus(); el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } }, 50)}
   737→          className="w-24 sm:w-28"
   738→        />
   739→        <div className="flex gap-0.5">
   740→          {SUFFIXES.map(s => (
   741→            <button
   742→              key={s}
   743→              type="button"
   744→              onClick={() => setForm(f => ({ ...f, suffix: f.suffix === s ? '' : s }))}
   745→              className={`px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono border ${form.suffix === s ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}
   746→              title={s}
   747→            >
   748→              {s.slice(1)}

any[];\n  const hasUniqueConstraint = srIndexes.some((idx: any) => {\n    if (!idx.unique) return false;\n    const cols = db.prepare(`PRAGMA index_info(\"${idx.name}\")`).all() as any[];\n    const colNames = cols.map((c: any) => c.name).sort();\n    return colNames.join(',') === 'exercise_id,operator_id,repeater_id';\n  });\n  if (hasUniqueConstraint) {\n    console.log('  Migration: dropping UNIQUE constraint on signal_reports (exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)');\n    db.exec(`\n      CREATE TABLE signal_reports_new (\n        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n        exercise_id TEXT REFERENCES exercises(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n        operator_id INTEGER REFERENCES operators(id),\n        repeater_id INTEGER REFERENCES repeaters(id),\n        readability INTEGER,\n        strength INTEGER,\n        db_over_s9 TEXT,\n        einstiegspunkt_id INTEGER REFERENCES einstiegspunkte(id),\n        is_op_marker INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n        notes TEXT,\n        entered_by TEXT,\n        created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n        updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n        suffix TEXT DEFAULT NULL,\n        bezirk_code TEXT REFERENCES bezirke(code)\n      );\n      INSERT INTO signal_reports_new SELECT id, exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability, strength, db_over_s9, einstiegspunkt_id, is_op_marker, notes, entered_by, created_at, updated_at, suffix, bezirk_code FROM signal_reports;\n      DROP TABLE signal_reports;\n      ALTER TABLE signal_reports_new RENAME TO signal_reports;\n      CREATE INDEX idx_signal_reports_exercise ON signal_reports(exercise_id);\n      CREATE INDEX idx_signal_reports_operator ON signal_reports(operator_id);\n      CREATE INDEX idx_signal_reports_repeater ON signal_reports(repeater_id);\n      CREATE INDEX idx_signal_reports_bezirk ON signal_reports(bezirk_code);\n    `);\n  }\n\n  // Migration: backfill operators with null bundesland_code using callsign prefix"
}

> AGENT

Build is clean. Here's a summary of the changes:

**1. `server/src/db/schema.ts`** — Added migration that detects the UNIQUE constraint on `(exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)` and recreates `signal_reports` without it, preserving all data and indexes.

**2. `server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** — Removed the `try/catch` around the POST reports insert that handled 409 UNIQUE constraint violations. The insert now always succeeds since duplicates are allowed.

**3. `client/src/components/BundMode.tsx`** — Simplified `handleBezirkSubmit` by removing the inner `try/catch` that caught 409 errors and patched existing reports. Now always creates a new report.

**4. `client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`** — Same simplification as BundMode.

     5→export default function LoginPage() {

    42→                required
    43→                autoFocus
    44→              />
    45→            </div>

    49→                type="password"
    50→                value={password}
    51→                onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}

    53→                required
    54→              />
    55→            </div>
    56→            {error && <p className="text-red-600 text-sm">{error}</p>}

     1→import { Routes, Route, Navigate, Link, Outlet } from 'react-router-dom';
     2→import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './hooks/useAuth';
     3→import Layout from './components/Layout';
     4→import LoginPage from './pages/LoginPage';
     5→import DashboardPage from './pages/DashboardPage';
     6→import ExercisePage from './pages/ExercisePage';
     7→import ExerciseSetupPage from './pages/ExerciseSetupPage';
     8→import OperatorsPage from './pages/OperatorsPage';
     9→import ReportsPage from './pages/ReportsPage';
    10→import AggregatedReportsPage from './pages/AggregatedReportsPage';
    11→import ImpressumPage from './pages/ImpressumPage';
    12→import DatenschutzPage from './pages/DatenschutzPage';
    13→import HilfePage from './pages/HilfePage';
    14→
    15→function ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    16→  const { token } = useAuth();
    17→  if (!token) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
    18→  return <>{children}</>;
    19→}
    20→
    21→function PublicLayout() {
    22→  return (
    23→    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col">
    24→      <nav className="bg-[#1e3a5f] text-white px-2 sm:px-4 py-2 flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-4 shadow-md">
    25→        <Link to="/login" className="hover:text-blue-200 flex-shrink-0">
    26→          <div className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold tracking-wide leading-tight">BOS-ARSA Log</div>
    27→        </Link>
    28→      </nav>
    29→      <main className="p-2 sm:p-4 max-w-[1600px] mx-auto flex-1 w-full">
    30→        <Outlet />
    31→      </main>
    32→      <footer className="bg-gray-100 border-t text-center py-3 text-xs text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 sm:gap-4 flex-wrap px-2">
    33→        <a href="https://oeradio.at" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="hover:text-gray-700">oeradio.at</a>
    34→        <span>·</span>
    35→        <a href="https://bosarsa.oeradio.at" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="hover:text-gray-700">bosarsa.oeradio.at</a>
    36→        <span>·</span>
    37→        <Link to="/impressum" className="hover:text-gray-700">Impressum</Link>
    38→        <span>·</span>
    39→        <Link to="/datenschutz" className="hover:text-gray-700">Datenschutz</Link>
    40→        <span>·</span>
    41→        <Link to="/hilfe" className="hover:text-gray-700">Hilfe</Link>
    42→        <span>·</span>
    43→        <a href="https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="hover:text-gray-700">GitHub</a>
    44→        <span>·</span>
    45→        <a href="https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/releases/tag/v1.1.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="hover:text-gray-700">v1.1.0</a>
    46→      </footer>
    47→    </div>
    48→  );
    49→}
    50→
    51→function FlexLayout() {
    52→  const { token } = useAuth();
    53→  return token ? <Layout /> : <PublicLayout />;
    54→}
    55→
    56→export default function App() {
    57→  return (
    58→    <AuthProvider>
    59→      <Routes>
    60→        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
    61→        <Route
    62→          path="/"
    63→          element={
    64→            <ProtectedRoute>
    65→              <Layout />
    66→            </ProtectedRoute>
    67→          }
    68→        >
    69→          <Route index element={<DashboardPage />} />
    70→          <Route path="exercises/:id" element={<ExercisePage />} />
    71→          <Route path="exercises/:id/setup" element={<ExerciseSetupPage />} />
    72→          <Route path="exercises/:id/reports" element={<ReportsPage />} />
    73→          <Route path="reports" element={<AggregatedReportsPage />} />
    74→          <Route path="operators" element={<OperatorsPage />} />
    75→        </Route>
    76→        <Route element={<FlexLayout />}>
    77→          <Route path="/impressum" element={<ImpressumPage />} />
    78→          <Route path="/datenschutz" element={<DatenschutzPage />} />
    79→          <Route path="/hilfe" element={<HilfePage />} />
    80→        </Route>
    81→      </Routes>
    82→    </AuthProvider>
    83→  );
    84→}

index a45f6bd..79cfeac 100644
--- a/server/src/db/schema.ts
+++ b/server/src/db/schema.ts
@@ -273,6 +273,44 @@ export function runMigrations(db: Database.Database): void {
     db.exec("ALTER TABLE exercise_repeaters ADD COLUMN home_repeater TEXT DEFAULT NULL");
   }
 
+  // Migration: drop UNIQUE constraint on signal_reports (allow duplicate callsigns per repeater)
+  const srIndexes = db.prepare("PRAGMA index_list(signal_reports)").all() as any[];
+  const hasUniqueConstraint = srIndexes.some((idx: any) => {
+    if (!idx.unique) return false;
+    const cols = db.prepare(`PRAGMA index_info("${idx.name}")`).all() as any[];
+    const colNames = cols.map((c: any) => c.name).sort();
+    return colNames.join(',') === 'exercise_id,operator_id,repeater_id';
+  });
+  if (hasUniqueConstraint) {
+    console.log('  Migration: dropping UNIQUE constraint on signal_reports (exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)');
+    db.exec(`
+      CREATE TABLE signal_reports_new (
+        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+        exercise_id TEXT REFERENCES exercises(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
+        operator_id INTEGER REFERENCES operators(id),
+        repeater_id INTEGER REFERENCES repeaters(id),
+        readability INTEGER,
+        strength INTEGER,
+        db_over_s9 TEXT,
+        einstiegspunkt_id INTEGER REFERENCES einstiegspunkte(id),
+        is_op_marker INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
+        notes TEXT,
+        entered_by TEXT,
+        created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
+        updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
+        suffix TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
+        bezirk_code TEXT REFERENCES bezirke(code)
+      );
+      INSERT INTO signal_reports_new SELECT id, exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability, strength, db_over_s9, einstiegspunkt_id, is_op_marker, notes, entered_by, created_at, updated_at, suffix, bezirk_code FROM signal_reports;
+      DROP TABLE signal_reports;
+      ALTER TABLE signal_reports_new RENAME TO signal_reports;
+      CREATE INDEX idx_signal_reports_exercise ON signal_reports(exercise_id);
+      CREATE INDEX idx_signal_reports_operator ON signal_reports(operator_id);
+      CREATE INDEX idx_signal_reports_repeater ON signal_reports(repeater_id);
+      CREATE INDEX idx_signal_reports_bezirk ON signal_reports(bezirk_code);
+    `);
+  }
+
   // Migration: backfill operators with null bundesland_code using callsign prefix
   const nullBlOps = db.prepare("SELECT id, callsign FROM operators WHERE bundesland_code IS NULL").all() as { id: number; callsign: string }[];
   if (nullBlOps.length > 0) {

     1→# BOS-ARSA Log Project Memory
     2→
     3→## Environment
     4→- NixOS system — `npm`/`node` not on PATH directly, use `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "..."` to run them
     5→- Monorepo: `/client` (React/Vite) + `/server` (Express/TypeScript)
     6→- SQLite database at `/data/bosarsalog.db`
     7→
     8→## Deployment
     9→- **Always use `./deploy-production.sh`** — deploys via SSH to remote host, builds Docker there
    10→- NEVER use `docker compose up` locally — that's not the production deploy path
    11→
    12→## Key Patterns
    13→- Schema migrations go in `server/src/db/schema.ts` using PRAGMA table_info checks
    14→- Seed data in `server/src/db/seed.ts` — rerun with `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run db:seed"`
    15→- API routes follow RESTful patterns in `server/src/routes/`
    16→- Frontend uses `apiFetch()` wrapper from `client/src/services/api.ts`
    17→- German UI, English code convention
    18→
    19→## Completed Features
    20→- **Login overhaul** (2026-02-15): Replaced callsign+PIN auth with username+password. Two users: `admin`/`BosArsa2024!` and `operator`/`Funk73!ops`. Schema: `admins` table uses `username`/`password_hash`. Role-based access on exercises routes. ExerciseSetupPage: name/start/BL-toggle admin-only.
    21→- **Landesweite restructuring** (2026-02-15): Added `bundesland_code` and `is_custom` columns to repeaters table. Expanded repeater list with comprehensive Austrian OEVSV data (~80 repeaters across OE1-OE9). ExerciseSetupPage restructured with Bundesland selection grid, per-BL repeater sections, custom repeater creation. LandMode tabs grouped by Bundesland with operator callsign badges.
    22→- **GUID exercises + status removal** (2026-02-15): Exercise IDs changed from INTEGER AUTOINCREMENT to TEXT UUIDs (crypto.randomUUID()). Removed exercise status concept entirely (no planned/active/completed). Schema migration detects INTEGER id and recreates all exercise-related tables. All client parseInt() calls removed, exerciseId prop type changed to string.
    23→- **home_repeater moved to BundMode** (2026-02-25): Moved from operators table (Stammdaten) to exercise_repeaters table (per-exercise). REP input in BundMode BL header next to OP. Socket event `home_repeater_updated`. operators.home_repeater column kept for DB compat but unused.
    24→

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/services/api.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function getOrCreateOperator(callsign: string, existing: any): Promise<any> {\n  if (existing) return existing;\n  if (!callsign || callsign.length < 3) return null;\n  try {\n    return await apiFetch('/api/v1/operators', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ callsign: callsign.toUpperCase() }),\n    });\n  } catch (err: any) {\n    if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {\n      const results = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?q=${encodeURIComponent(callsign)}`);\n      return results.find((o: any) => o.callsign === callsign.toUpperCase()) || results[0] || null;\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function getOrCreateOperator(callsign: string, existing: any, extras?: { name?: string; qth?: string }): Promise<any> {\n  if (existing) return existing;\n  if (!callsign || callsign.length < 3) return null;\n  try {\n    return await apiFetch('/api/v1/operators', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ callsign: callsign.toUpperCase(), name: extras?.name || null, qth: extras?.qth || null }),\n    });\n  } catch (err: any) {\n    if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {\n      const results = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?q=${encodeURIComponent(callsign)}`);\n      return results.find((o: any) => o.callsign === callsign.toUpperCase()) || results[0] || null;\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n}"
}

> AGENT

Build clean. The bug was:

1. **New operators**: `getOrCreateOperator` only sent `callsign` — name/QTH were discarded. Now passes them to the POST.
2. **Existing operators** (selected from autocomplete): `handleBezirkSubmit` never patched name/QTH even if the user edited those fields. Now patches if they differ.

Both BundMode and LandMode are fixed.

> DEVELOPER

deploy

> AGENT

Found it. Line 277 — the POST response query has `o.name as operator_name` but is missing `o.qth as operator_qth`. The GET query (line 55) has it. So the report appears without QTH until page reload.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01TAaYzpJizyNX4xzJHsJWEN
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts",
  "old_string": "    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,\n      r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,\n      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id\n    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id\n    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id\n    WHERE sr.id = ?\n  `).get(result.lastInsertRowid);",
  "new_string": "    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.qth as operator_qth, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,\n      r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,\n      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id\n    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id\n    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id\n    WHERE sr.id = ?\n  `).get(result.lastInsertRowid);"
}

     1→# BOS-ARSA Log Project Memory
     2→
     3→## Environment
     4→- NixOS system — `npm`/`node` not on PATH directly, use `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "..."` to run them
     5→- Monorepo: `/client` (React/Vite) + `/server` (Express/TypeScript)
     6→- SQLite database at `/data/bosarsalog.db`
     7→
     8→## Deployment
     9→- **Always use `./deploy-production.sh`** — deploys via SSH to remote host, builds Docker there
    10→- NEVER use `docker compose up` locally — that's not the production deploy path
    11→
    12→## Key Patterns
    13→- Schema migrations go in `server/src/db/schema.ts` using PRAGMA table_info checks
    14→- Seed data in `server/src/db/seed.ts` — rerun with `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run db:seed"`
    15→- API routes follow RESTful patterns in `server/src/routes/`
    16→- Frontend uses `apiFetch()` wrapper from `client/src/services/api.ts`
    17→- German UI, English code convention
    18→
    19→## Completed Features
    20→- **Login overhaul** (2026-02-15): Replaced callsign+PIN auth with username+password. Two users: `admin`/`BosArsa2024!` and `operator`/`Funk73!ops`. Schema: `admins` table uses `username`/`password_hash`. Role-based access on exercises routes. ExerciseSetupPage: name/start/BL-toggle admin-only.
    21→- **Landesweite restructuring** (2026-02-15): Added `bundesland_code` and `is_custom` columns to repeaters table. Expanded repeater list with comprehensive Austrian OEVSV data (~80 repeaters across OE1-OE9). ExerciseSetupPage restructured with Bundesland selection grid, per-BL repeater sections, custom repeater creation. LandMode tabs grouped by Bundesland with operator callsign badges.
    22→- **GUID exercises + status removal** (2026-02-15): Exercise IDs changed from INTEGER AUTOINCREMENT to TEXT UUIDs (crypto.randomUUID()). Removed exercise status concept entirely (no planned/active/completed). Schema migration detects INTEGER id and recreates all exercise-related tables. All client parseInt() calls removed, exerciseId prop type changed to string.
    23→- **home_repeater moved to BundMode** (2026-02-25): Moved from operators table (Stammdaten) to exercise_repeaters table (per-exercise). REP input in BundMode BL header next to OP. Socket event `home_repeater_updated`. operators.home_repeater column kept for DB compat but unused.
    24→- **Duplicate callsigns allowed** (2026-02-25, v1.1.1): Dropped `UNIQUE(exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)` constraint on `signal_reports` via migration (PRAGMA index_list detection → recreate table). Removed 409 conflict handling from POST `/reports` endpoint. Removed client-side try/catch 409→PATCH fallback in BundMode and LandMode `handleBezirkSubmit`. Same callsign can now appear multiple times per repeater per exercise.
    25→

  "new_string": "- **Duplicate callsigns allowed** (2026-02-25, v1.1.1): Dropped `UNIQUE(exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)` constraint on `signal_reports` via migration (PRAGMA index_list detection → recreate table). Removed 409 conflict handling from POST `/reports` endpoint. Removed client-side try/catch 409→PATCH fallback in BundMode and LandMode `handleBezirkSubmit`. Same callsign can now appear multiple times per repeater per exercise.\n- **QTH/name on submit fix** (2026-02-25): `getOrCreateOperator()` now accepts optional `{ name, qth }` extras and passes them on POST create. `handleBezirkSubmit` in BundMode/LandMode patches operator name/QTH if filled. Also added `o.qth as operator_qth` to POST `/reports` response query (was missing, so QTH didn't show until page reload).\n\n## Known Pitfalls\n- **POST response queries must match GET queries**: When adding fields to the GET exercise/reports queries, also update the POST and PATCH response queries in `exercises.ts` — otherwise new data won't appear until page reload. Check all SELECT queries that return report objects."
}

> DEVELOPER

update memory with the qth fix

> AGENT

Memory is already up to date — I added the QTH/name fix entry and the "POST response queries must match GET queries" pitfall in the previous memory update. Here's what's there:

- **QTH/name on submit fix** (2026-02-25): `getOrCreateOperator()` now accepts optional `{ name, qth }` extras and passes them on POST create. `handleBezirkSubmit` in BundMode/LandMode patches operator name/QTH if filled. Also added `o.qth as operator_qth` to POST `/reports` response query (was missing, so QTH didn't show until page reload).

- **Known Pitfalls → POST response queries must match GET queries**: When adding fields to the GET exercise/reports queries, also update the POST and PATCH response queries in `exercises.ts`.

```
     1→# BOS-ARSA Log
     2→
     3→**Amateurfunk Notfunkübung — Live-Erfassung, Multi-User, Auswertung**
     4→
     5→A real-time, multi-user web application for BOS-ARSA amateur radio exercises across Austria. Multiple operators simultaneously log signal reports from ~100 ham radio stations on repeaters and direct frequencies. BOS-ARSA Log replaces the shared Excel workbook and standalone HTML prototypes with a single unified tool.
     6→
     7→Part of the [oeradio.at](https://oeradio.at) ecosystem.
     8→
     9→## Tech Stack
    10→
    11→| Layer | Technology |
    12→|-------|-----------|
    13→| Frontend | React 18, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, Socket.IO client |
    14→| Backend | Node.js, Express, TypeScript, Socket.IO server |
    15→| Database | SQLite via better-sqlite3 |
    16→| Auth | Username/password with bcrypt + JWT tokens |
    17→| Security | Helmet, rate limiting, CORS restriction, non-root Docker |
    18→| Deployment | Docker, Docker Compose, Cloudflare Tunnel |
    19→
    20→## Architecture
    21→
    22→```
    23→/client          React SPA (Vite)
    24→/server          Express API + WebSocket server
    25→/data            SQLite database (Docker volume-mounted)
    26→```
    27→
    28→- RESTful API for CRUD operations, WebSocket (Socket.IO) for real-time sync between concurrent users
    29→- Server serves the built client in production
    30→- SQLite keeps deployment simple — the data volume is small (~1,000 operators, ~200 reports/week)
    31→- SQLite tuned with WAL mode, `synchronous=NORMAL`, 64MB page cache, in-memory temp store
    32→- Critical multi-statement operations (report+attendance, exercise delete) wrapped in transactions
    33→
    34→## Key Domain Concepts
    35→
    36→| Concept | Description |
    37→|---------|-------------|
    38→| **Operator (Rufzeichen)** | Ham radio callsign, e.g. OE8YML, S55UJE |
    39→| **Repeater/Umsetzer** | Radio relay station with TX frequency, offset, CTCSS |
    40→| **Linked Repeater** | One logical repeater spanning multiple physical sites (Einstiegspunkte) |
    41→| **Einstiegspunkt** | Physical entry point in a linked repeater network, e.g. Hermannskogel (HK) |
    42→| **Signal Report (Rapport)** | RST format, e.g. "5/9+30 HK" = R5 / S9+30dB via Hermannskogel |
    43→| **Exercise (Übung)** | One session with configured repeaters and collected reports |
    44→| **Bezirk** | Austrian political district, identified by 2-3 letter code |
    45→| **Bundesland** | Austrian state (OE1=Wien through OE9=Vorarlberg) |
    46→| **Nachbarland** | Neighbor country (SI, CZ, DE, IT, SK, HR, LI, CH, HU) |
    47→
    48→## Features
    49→
    50→### Dashboard
    51→
    52→- **Year filter** — Button row to switch between years (2026, 2027, ...), derived from existing data
    53→- **Type filter** — Checkbox filter by exercise type (KriKom, Notfunk, etc.), appears when multiple types exist in a year
    54→- Summary cards, trend chart, and exercise table filtered by selected year and type
    55→- Exercise table shows type (short label), name, participants, and reports with quarterly subtotals
    56→
    57→### Two Data Entry Modes
    58→
    59→- **Frequenzen (Land)** — Repeater-first entry. Select a repeater, enter reports grouped by Bezirk. Each repeater card includes a "?? Sonstige" row for operators from outside the repeater's Bundesland.
    60→- **OE-Link (Bund)** — Geography-first entry. Bundesland/Bezirk hierarchy with linked repeater selection per district. Reports stay in the Bundesland where they were entered, regardless of the repeater's physical location. Each Bundesland includes a "?? Sonstige" row. Only available when OE-Link mode is enabled for the exercise. Linked repeaters are automatically added when OE-Link is toggled on and are hidden from the Frequenzen setup.
    61→
    62→### Exercise Setup
    63→
    64→- **Exercise type** selection (Krisenkommunikationsübung, 80m Notfunk Runde, or custom free-text types)
    65→- Optional exercise name (e.g. "Sonntagsrunde KW 07")
    66→- Bundesland selection grid (OE1–OE9) with one-click activation of all repeaters per state
    67→- ~80 pre-configured Austrian repeaters from OEVSV data
    68→- Custom repeater and simplex frequency creation
    69→- OE-Link toggle for linked repeater mode
    70→- Per-repeater operator (OP) callsign assignment
    71→- Auto-save with visual status indicator (Speichern / Gespeichert / Fehler)
    72→- **Exercise deletion** with danger-styled confirmation dialog (admin only)
    73→
    74→### Interactive Austria Map with Neighbor Countries
    75→
    76→- Choropleth map of Austria (OE1–OE9) with participant/report density
    77→- 9 neighbor countries displayed: Germany, Czech Republic, Slovakia, Hungary, Slovenia, Italy, Switzerland, Liechtenstein, Croatia
    78→- High-resolution borders from Natural Earth 10m data
    79→- Countries with participating operators are color-coded by activity level
    80→- Hover tooltips showing participant count and report count per region
    81→
    82→### Automatic Callsign-to-Country Assignment
    83→
    84→- Foreign callsign prefixes automatically mapped to the correct country (e.g. S55UJE → Slovenia, DL1ABC → Germany)
    85→- Supported prefixes: OE1–OE9, S5 (Slovenia), OK/OL (Czech Republic), DA-DR (Germany), I (Italy), OM (Slovakia), 9A (Croatia), HB0 (Liechtenstein), HB (Switzerland), HA/HG (Hungary)
    86→- Strips portable/mobile suffixes (/P, /M, /OE8) before matching
    87→- Auto-derived on operator creation; existing operators backfilled via migration
    88→
    89→### Live Multi-User
    90→
    91→- Socket.IO real-time sync — reports entered by one user appear instantly for all others
    92→- Running totals bar always visible: participant count, total reports, per-repeater counts
    93→- Optimized for speed: autofocus, enter-to-submit, auto-uppercase, keyboard shortcuts
    94→- **Two-step Enter flow** — Enter in the callsign field jumps to the rapport field (cursor at end); Enter in the rapport field submits the report
    95→- Repeater headers show callsign in parentheses (e.g. "Laaerberg 2m (OE1XFW)")
    96→- OE-Link Bundesland headers with OE prefix (e.g. "OE1 Wien", "OE8 Kärnten")
    97→
    98→### Keyboard Shortcuts
    99→
   100→| Shortcut | Action |
   101→|----------|--------|
   102→| `Alt+1` through `Alt+9` | Insert "5/1" through "5/9" in rapport field |
   103→| `Ctrl+1` through `Ctrl+9` | Insert "OE1" through "OE9" in callsign field |
   104→| `Enter` (in callsign) | Jump to rapport field |
   105→| `Enter` (in rapport/notes) | Submit report and clear fields |
   106→| `Arrow Up/Down` | Navigate autocomplete suggestions |
   107→| `Escape` | Close autocomplete dropdown |
   108→
   109→### Reports & Export
   110→
   111→- **Per-exercise reports** — Summary with per-repeater and per-Bezirk/Bundesland/Land breakdown
   112→- **Auswertung (Aggregated Reports)** — Date-range aggregated statistics across multiple exercises
   113→  - Quick-select buttons: Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4, full year
   114→  - Custom date range (Von / Bis) picker
   115→  - Exercise type filter (multi-select checkboxes: KriKom, Notfunk, etc.)
   116→  - Summary cards: unique participants, total reports, exercise count, average participants per exercise
   117→  - Collapsible exercise list with per-exercise breakdown
   118→  - Deduplicates participants across exercises for accurate totals
   119→- Interactive Austria map with neighbor countries showing participant distribution
   120→- Bar chart (stations vs. reports) and pie chart (distribution by Bundesland/Land)
   121→- Participants list with callsign, name, location, report count
   122→- Export formats:
   123→  - **PNG** — Full-page screenshot of the analytics view
   124→  - **ADIF** — Amateur Data Interchange Format (.adi) for import into QRZ.com, LOTW, and other logbook software
   125→  - **TXT OE-Link** — Bundesland-grouped text export
   126→  - **TXT Frequenzen** — Repeater-grouped text export
   127→  - **TXT Kombiniert** — Summary statistics with per-repeater breakdown
   128→
   129→### Bezirk Assignment
   130→
   131→- **Operator bezirk** (home location) and **report bezirk** (where the rapport was given) are independent
   132→- Operator home bezirk is auto-derived from QTH address when possible (matching against district names)
   133→- Report bezirk is stored per signal report from the entry row context
   134→- An operator can travel and report from any bezirk without affecting their registered home location
   135→- All stats, exports, and charts group by the report's bezirk
   136→
   137→### Operator Registry
   138→
   139→- Searchable operator database (callsign, name, QTH)
   140→- Quick-add during live entry when callsign not found
   141→- Inline editing of operator details (Name, QTH) directly from report entry forms
   142→- Operator Name and QTH displayed alongside callsign in all report lists
   143→- Paginated browsing (50 per page)
   144→- Bulk import from official Fernmeldebehörde Rufzeichenliste PDF (see below)
   145→
   146→### Security
   147→
   148→- Helmet security headers (CSP, HSTS, X-Frame-Options)
   149→- Rate limiting on login (10 attempts per 15 minutes)
   150→- CORS restricted to configured origin
   151→- JWT secret fail-fast in production
   152→- Request body size limit (1MB)
   153→- Input length validation
   154→- Docker container runs as non-root user
   155→- Port bound to localhost only (Cloudflare tunnel access)
   156→
   157→### User Roles
   158→
   159→| Capability | Admin | Erfasser |
   160→|-----------|-------|----------|
   161→| Create exercises | ✓ | — |
   162→| Delete exercises | ✓ | — |
   163→| Configure repeaters | ✓ | — |
   164→| Change exercise type/name/date | ✓ | — |
   165→| Enable OE-Link | ✓ | — |
   166→| Enter/edit/delete reports | ✓ | ✓ |
   167→| Manage operators | ✓ | ✓ |
   168→| View analytics & export | ✓ | ✓ |
   169→
   170→## Getting Started
   171→
   172→### Prerequisites
   173→
   174→- Node.js 20+
   175→- npm
   176→
   177→### Development
   178→
   179→```bash
   180→# Install dependencies
   181→npm install
   182→cd server && npm install && cd ..
   183→cd client && npm install && cd ..
   184→
   185→# Seed the database with reference data (idempotent — safe to run multiple times)
   186→npm run db:seed
   187→
   188→# Start both client and server in dev mode
   189→npm run dev
   190→```
   191→
   192→The client runs on `http://localhost:5173` with API proxy to `http://localhost:3000`.
   193→
   194→### Production (Docker)
   195→
   196→```bash
   197→# Build and run
   198→docker compose up -d
   199→
   200→# Seed database (first run only — idempotent, won't overwrite existing data)
   201→docker exec bosarsalog node dist/server/db/seed.js
   202→```
   203→
   204→Required environment variables:
   205→
   206→| Variable | Description |
   207→|----------|-------------|
   208→| `JWT_SECRET` | Random 64+ char secret (required in production) |
   209→| `CORS_ORIGIN` | Allowed origin URL (default: `https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at`) |
   210→| `DATABASE_PATH` | SQLite DB path (default: `/data/bosarsalog.db`) |
   211→
   212→## API
   213→
   214→All endpoints are prefixed with `/api/v1/`. Protected routes require `Authorization: Bearer <token>`.
   215→
   216→### Auth
   217→
   218→```
   219→POST /api/v1/auth/login    { username, password } -> { token, admin }
   220→```
   221→
   222→### Exercises
   223→
   224→```
   225→GET    /api/v1/exercises              List all (with summary stats)
   226→POST   /api/v1/exercises              Create { date, name, exercise_type }
   227→GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id          Full exercise with reports
   228→PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id          Update (name, exercise_type, organisator, oe_link)
   229→DELETE /api/v1/exercises/:id          Delete exercise + all related data (admin)
   230→GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/stats    Live statistics
   231→GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports  All signal reports
   232→POST   /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports  Create report
   233→PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports/:rid  Edit report
   234→DELETE /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports/:rid  Delete report
   235→```
   236→
   237→### Exercise Repeaters
   238→
   239→```
   240→GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters
   241→POST   /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters      Activate repeater
   242→PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid  Update OP assignment
   243→DELETE /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid  Deactivate repeater
   244→```
   245→
   246→### Operators
   247→
   248→```
   249→GET    /api/v1/operators         List/search (?q= for autocomplete)
   250→POST   /api/v1/operators         Quick-add new operator
   251→GET    /api/v1/operators/:id     Full profile with history
   252→PATCH  /api/v1/operators/:id     Update operator
   253→```
   254→
   255→### Reference Data
   256→
   257→```
   258→GET /api/v1/reference/bundeslaender
   259→GET /api/v1/reference/bezirke
   260→GET /api/v1/reference/einstiegspunkte
   261→GET /api/v1/repeaters
   262→GET /api/v1/repeaters/:id/einstiegspunkte
   263→```
   264→
   265→### Aggregated Reports
   266→
   267→```
   268→GET /api/v1/reports/stats?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD[&types=...]   Aggregated stats (optional type filter)
   269→```
   270→
   271→### Export
   272→
   273→```
   274→GET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/bund      TXT (Bundesland-grouped)
   275→GET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/land      TXT (Repeater-grouped)
   276→GET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/combined  Summary report
   277→```
   278→
   279→### WebSocket Events (Socket.IO)
   280→
   281→```
   282→Client -> Server:
   283→  join_exercise      { exercise_id }
   284→  leave_exercise     { exercise_id }
   285→  report_created     { report data }
   286→  report_updated     { report data }
   287→  report_deleted     { report_id }
   288→  attendance_updated { attendance data }
   289→
   290→Server -> Client (broadcast):
   291→  report_created     { report, entered_by }
   292→  report_updated     { report, entered_by }
   293→  report_deleted     { report_id, entered_by }
   294→  attendance_updated { attendance, entered_by }
   295→```
   296→
   297→## Rufzeichen Import (Fernmeldebehörde PDF)
   298→
   299→Import operators from the official Austrian Rufzeichenliste PDF published by the Fernmeldebüro:
   300→
   301→```bash
   302→# Update mode (default) — insert new, update existing name/qth
   303→RUFZEICHEN_MODE=update npm run import:rufzeichen -- /path/to/Rufzeichenliste.pdf
   304→
   305→# Skip mode — insert new only, leave existing untouched
   306→RUFZEICHEN_MODE=skip npm run import:rufzeichen -- /path/to/Rufzeichenliste.pdf
   307→```
   308→
   309→Requires `pdftotext` (poppler-utils). On NixOS: `nix-shell -p nodejs poppler-utils --run "..."`.
   310→
   311→The script parses callsign, name, and Standort (QTH) from the PDF table. Bundesland is auto-derived from the callsign prefix. Redacted entries (`*-*-*`) are skipped. The import is idempotent.
   312→
   313→## Seed Data
   314→
   315→The seed script (`npm run db:seed`) is **idempotent** — it only inserts data if the tables are empty, and never deletes existing data. It populates:
   316→
   317→- **18 Bundesländer** — 9 Austrian states + 9 international entries
   318→- **84 Bezirke** — Austrian political districts with capital flags
   319→- **~80 Repeaters** — Comprehensive OEVSV data across OE1–OE9 plus international and simplex
   320→- **12 Einstiegspunkte** — Entry points for the Magdalensberg linked system
   321→- **2 Users** — Admin and operator accounts
   322→
   323→## Conventions
   324→
   325→- German UI text, English code
   326→- Callsigns always uppercase
   327→- RST format: R/S with optional +dB modifier and location suffix
   328→- All timestamps in Europe/Vienna timezone
   329→- API prefix: `/api/v1/`
   330→- Bezirk badges: Landeshauptstadt = red, Statutarstadt/normal = gray, Sonstige (??) = yellow
   331→- Bundesland headers with blue badges
   332→- All 93 Austrian Bezirke with "C" suffix convention for Landeshauptstädte (e.g. GC=Graz, IC=Innsbruck)
   333→
   334→## License
   335→
   336→MIT
   337→

</system-reminder>
