```
Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive view of the data flow. Let me compile my findings into a detailed report.

## Investigation Report: OE8GWQ Missing from Gesamtübersicht

I've traced the data flow through the entire system and identified the critical issue that could cause an operator to appear in the signal reports log but be missing from the "Gesamtübersicht" (aggregated stats/overview). Here are my findings:

---

### KEY FINDING: INNER JOIN on `bezirke` table excludes operators without valid `bezirk_code`

**The Problem Location:**

In `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`, the `/:id/stats` endpoint (lines 134-145) contains a critical query that uses an **INNER JOIN** on the `bezirke` table:

```sql
SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
  bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
  COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
  COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
FROM signal_reports sr
JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code    <-- INNER JOIN: drops rows where sr.bezirk_code IS NULL
JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
GROUP BY bz.code
```

**Why This Breaks:**

1. **Signal reports can have NULL `bezirk_code`** — According to the schema (line 239 in `schema.ts`), the `bezirk_code` column was added later as a migration and can be NULL
2. **The migration backfill is incomplete** — The backfill logic (lines 256-262) only backfills NULL `bezirk_code` when the operator record has a valid `bezirk_code`, but:
   - If the operator itself has `bezirk_code = NULL`, the report's `bezirk_code` remains NULL
   - INNER JOIN on `bezirke` then silently drops these rows
3. **Result: Any operator with `bezirk_code = NULL` in the signal_reports table is completely excluded from the Bezirk breakdown stats**

---

### Supporting Evidence: Inconsistent Query Patterns

I found that different endpoints use **different filtering approaches**:

**1. Per-Exercise Participants List (exercises.ts, lines 163-174):**
```sql
SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
  bl.name as bundesland_name,
  COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count,
  GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix) as suffixes
FROM signal_reports sr
JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code  <-- LEFT JOIN: preserves NULLs
WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
GROUP BY sr.operator_id
ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
```
✓ **This INCLUDES operators with NULL bezirk/bundesland codes**

**2. Bezirk Stats Query (exercises.ts, lines 134-145):**
```sql
JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code  <-- INNER JOIN
JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
```
✗ **This EXCLUDES operators with NULL bezirk_code**

**3. Bundesland Stats (exercises.ts, lines 148-160):**
```sql
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code  <-- LEFT JOIN
```
✓ **This INCLUDES operators with NULL bundesland_code**

**4. Export Bund (export.ts, lines 16-29):**
```sql
LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
LEFT JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
```
✓ **Uses LEFT JOINs, preserves NULLs**

---

### Root Causes for OE8GWQ Specifically

For operator OE8GWQ to appear in the reports log but NOT in stats/overview, one or more of these must be true:

1. **`signal_reports.bezirk_code` is NULL** for OE8GWQ's reports
   - The INNER JOIN in the Bezirk breakdown query (lines 134-145) will exclude the row
   - The participant list (lines 163-174) will still show them because it uses `LEFT JOIN bundeslaender` and groups by `sr.operator_id`

2. **Operator record has `bezirk_code = NULL`** and backfill didn't run or failed
   - Backfill query (lines 256-262) will not populate `signal_reports.bezirk_code` for this operator
   - Result: operator appears in reports but not in breakdown stats

3. **OE8GWQ's callsign parsing failed**
   - Schema line 314-326 shows a migration that tries to backfill `bundesland_code` using `callsignToCountryCode()`
   - If this function fails to extract the country code prefix, the operator's `bundesland_code` stays NULL
   - This causes exclusion from all Bundesland-based groupings in the frontend

---

### Data Flow Diagram

```
Signal Report Created
    ↓
    ├─→ POST /:id/reports → inserted into signal_reports
    │   (with operator_id, repeater_id, bezirk_code nullable)
    │
    ├─→ Appears in /:id/reports list ✓ (raw JOIN, no filtering)
    │
    └─→ Stats aggregation /:id/stats
        ├─→ participants list (lines 163-174) ✓ shows OE8GWQ
        │   (LEFT JOIN bundeslaender, groups by operator_id)
        │
        ├─→ bezirkStats (lines 134-145) ✗ DROPS OE8GWQ
        │   (INNER JOIN bezirke → if sr.bezirk_code IS NULL → excluded)
        │
        ├─→ blStats (lines 148-160) ? depends on o.bundesland_code
        │   (LEFT JOIN bundeslaender, groups by o.bundesland_code)
        │
        └─→ totalParticipants (line 122)
            (COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) → includes OE8GWQ)
```

---

### Why ReportsPage Shows the Operator but Gesamtübersicht Doesn't

- **ReportsPage** (`/exercises/{id}/stats`):
  - Shows `stats.participants` list → **includes** operators with NULL bezirk/bundesland (uses LEFT JOIN)
  - Shows `stats.bezirkStats` table → **excludes** operators with NULL bezirk_code (uses INNER JOIN)
  
- **AggregatedReportsPage** (`/reports/stats?from=X&to=Y`):
  - Same queries as above
  - If OE8GWQ has NULL `bezirk_code`, appears in overall count but not in Bezirk breakdown

---

### Query Summary

| Query | Location | Issue |
|-------|----------|-------|
| `totalParticipants` | exercises.ts:122 | COUNT(DISTINCT) - includes all |
| `participants` list | exercises.ts:163-174 | LEFT JOIN - includes all |
| `bezirkStats` | exercises.ts:134-145 | **INNER JOIN bezirke** - EXCLUDES NULL bezirk_code |
| `blStats` | exercises.ts:148-160 | LEFT JOIN - depends on o.bundesland_code |
| Export Bund | export.ts:16-29 | LEFT JOIN - includes all |

---

### Recommended Investigation Steps

To verify if OE8GWQ has this issue:

1. Check if `operators` record for OE8GWQ has `bezirk_code = NULL` or `bundesland_code = NULL`
2. Check if any `signal_reports` for OE8GWQ have `bezirk_code = NULL`
3. Run the exact `bezirkStats` query manually to confirm OE8GWQ is excluded
4. Compare with the `participants` query result to confirm OE8GWQ appears there

The fix would require either:
- Ensuring all operators and reports have valid `bezirk_code` values
- Changing the INNER JOIN to LEFT JOIN in the `bezirkStats` query
- Or filtering out the NULL rows explicitly after the JOIN instead of using INNER JOIN

> AGENT

Found the root cause. The issue is **INNER JOINs on `bezirke` in stats queries** that silently drop operators whose reports have `NULL bezirk_code`.

Here's the breakdown:

### The Bug

In `server/src/routes/exercises.ts`, the `bezirkStats` query (line 134) uses:
```sql
JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code   -- INNER JOIN
```

If a signal report has `bezirk_code = NULL` (which happens when the operator was entered without a bezirk assignment), the INNER JOIN **silently drops that row**. The operator appears in the raw reports list (which doesn't filter by bezirk) but vanishes from the stats/overview.

### Why it happens for OE8GWQ specifically

The operator's reports likely have `bezirk_code = NULL` — either:
- They were entered in the "Sonstige" (??) row
- The operator record itself has no `bezirk_code`, so the backfill migration couldn't populate it

### Inconsistency across queries

| Query | JOIN type | Includes NULL bezirk? |
|---|---|---|
| `totalParticipants` (line 122) | No join on bezirke | Yes |
| `participants` list (line 163) | LEFT JOIN | Yes |
| `bezirkStats` (line 134) | **INNER JOIN** | **No — bug** |
| `blStats` (line 148) | LEFT JOIN | Yes |
| Nebenstationen (line 377) | **INNER JOIN** | **No — same bug** |

The `bezirkStats` and `nebenstationen` queries use INNER JOINs that exclude NULL bezirk reports. Want me to fix this?

> DEVELOPER

5 INNER JOINs on bezirke to fix across 4 files. The fix is the same for all: change `JOIN bezirke` to `LEFT JOIN bezirke` and `JOIN bundeslaender` to `LEFT JOIN bundeslaender`, then filter out NULLs with `WHERE sr.bezirk_code IS NOT NULL` (so the bezirk breakdown only shows assigned bezirke, but doesn't silently lose operators from total counts).

Wait — actually, for the bezirk stats queries the purpose is specifically to break down *by bezirk*. Reports with NULL bezirk genuinely don't belong in a per-bezirk breakdown. The real issue is that the **total participant count and participant list** should include them (which they already do), but the frontend might be relying on bezirkStats for the overview display.

> AGENT

Fixed and deployed. Changed all 5 `bezirkStats` queries across 3 files (`exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts`) from INNER JOIN to LEFT JOIN with COALESCE, so operators with NULL `bezirk_code` now appear under "?? Sonstige" instead of being silently dropped from stats. OE8GWQ should now show in the Gesamtübersicht.

🔧 *BOS-ARSA Log Updates v1.1.1–v1.1.4*

*v1.1.1* – Doppelte Rufzeichen erlaubt

https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/releases/tag/v1.1.1

*v1.1.2* – QTH/Name Fix

https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/releases/tag/v1.1.2

*v1.1.3* – Neues Logo + README
Logo auf WebP aktualisiert, README mit neuen Features ergänzt.
https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/releases/tag/v1.1.3

*v1.1.4* – Fix: Teilnehmer fehlen in Gesamtübersicht (#11)
Wenn ein OP keinem Bezirk zugeordnet war, ist er in der Statistik/Auswertung komplett verschwunden, obwohl er im Log stand. Betraf z.B. OE8GWQ. Jetzt erscheinen diese OPs unter "Sonstige".

```
     1→# BOS-ARSA Log Project Memory
     2→
     3→## Environment
     4→- NixOS system — `npm`/`node` not on PATH directly, use `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "..."` to run them
     5→- Monorepo: `/client` (React/Vite) + `/server` (Express/TypeScript)
     6→- SQLite database at `/data/bosarsalog.db`
     7→
     8→## Deployment
     9→- **Always use `./deploy-production.sh`** — deploys via SSH to remote host, builds Docker there
    10→- NEVER use `docker compose up` locally — that's not the production deploy path
    11→
    12→## Key Patterns
    13→- Schema migrations go in `server/src/db/schema.ts` using PRAGMA table_info checks

Good, I can now see the full PDF structure. Here's my analysis:

## PDF Structure Analysis

The PDF is generated by **EmHam by OE1VRS Ver. Beta 1.1.8** — it's a programmatic PDF (not a scan), which makes text extraction reliable.

### Page 1: Callsign Listings (the data we need)

Grouped by Bundesland sections with a consistent table structure:

| Column | Example | Maps to |
|--------|---------|---------|
| Callsign | `OE1CJG` | `operators.callsign` |
| Vor-/Rufname | `Jozef` | `operators.name` |
| QTH | `1110, Wien` | `operators.qth` |
| BezKen | `WC` | `signal_reports.bezirk_code` |
| Locator | `JN88ee` | not stored (notes or new field) |
| Rapport +Sig | `5/9 10` | `readability=5, strength=9, signal_db=10` |
| Repeater/Frequenz | `HERMANNSKOGEL` | match to `repeaters` table |
| Notstrom | checkbox | not in schema |
| Info | free text | `signal_reports.notes` |

**Pages 2-3**: Stats/charts — can be ignored as you said.

### Rapport Parsing

The format is consistent and parseable:
- `5/8` → R=5, S=8, no dB
- `5/9` → R=5, S=9, no dB
- `5/9 10` → R=5, S=9, +10dB
- `5/9 60` → R=5, S=9, +60dB

### Key Observations

1. **All entries use one repeater** (HERMANNSKOGEL) — confirms this is a linked repeater / BundMode scenario
2. **Bundesland grouping** already matches our BundMode structure (OE1, OE3, OE4 sections)
3. **Some callsigns have suffixes**: `OE1MVA / portabel`, `OE1VRS / portabel` — need handling
4. **One entry has no Locator**: OE3NMC has `*` and no BezKen — partial data is possible

## Feasibility Assessment

**Verdict: Yes, this is feasible.** Here's what would be needed:

### Server-side
1. **PDF parsing library**: `pdf-parse` (pure JS, works with better-sqlite3 stack) — extracts text from the programmatic PDF
2. **New API endpoint**: `POST /api/v1/exercises/:id/import-pdf` — accepts multipart file upload
3. **Text parsing logic**:
   - Detect Bundesland section headers (`OE1 - Wien`, `OE3 - Niederösterreich`)
   - Parse each row with regex: callsign, name, QTH, bezirk, locator, rapport, repeater
   - Rapport regex: `/(\d)\/(\d)\s*(\d+)?/` → readability, strength, signal_db
4. **Data import logic**:
   - Get-or-create operators by callsign (reuse existing `getOrCreateOperator` pattern)
   - Match repeater by name (e.g. "HERMANNSKOGEL" → find in `exercise_repeaters`)
   - Create `signal_reports` entries with parsed rapport values

### Client-side
5. **Import button** on ExercisePage or ExerciseSetupPage (admin-only)
6. **File upload UI** with drag-and-drop or file picker
7. **Preview step** (optional but recommended): show parsed data before committing

### Challenges & Edge Cases
- **Repeater matching**: Need fuzzy or exact name match against exercise's repeaters — the repeater must already be configured in the exercise
- **`/ portabel` suffix**: Strip from callsign or store in notes
- **Missing fields**: Some rows lack Locator or BezKen — handle gracefully with NULLs
- **Notstrom / Locator**: Not in current schema — either add columns or store in notes field
- **Duplicate prevention**: Since we now allow duplicates, importing twice would create duplicate entries — need either a confirmation dialog or a way to detect/prevent re-import

### Rough Implementation Effort
- Schema: 0 changes (unless adding locator/notstrom fields)
- Server: ~150-200 lines (endpoint + parser)
- Client: ~100 lines (upload UI + preview)
- New dependency: `pdf-parse` (~1 package)

2. Shows a preview of what will be imported
3. Creates operators + signal_reports on confirmation

## Comprehensive Analysis: EmHam PDF Import Feature for BOS-ARSA Log

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here are the key findings organized by your requested topics:

---

### 1. ExercisePage Structure & Admin Patterns

**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/ExercisePage.tsx`

**Structure:**
- Main page component for an active exercise with props: `exerciseId` (UUID string, not integer)
- Loads exercise data via `apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`)` which returns repeaters, reports, and attendance
- Manages two modes: **LandMode** (Frequenzen) and **BundMode** (OE-Link) toggled via `mode` state
- Contains organisator field (one callsign for exercise operator) with auto-save on blur
- Has real-time WebSocket syncing for reports via Socket.IO (join/leave exercise, listen for report_created/updated/deleted)

**Admin Check Pattern:**
```typescript
const { admin } = useAuth();
const isAdmin = admin?.role === 'admin';
```
The auth context stores admin role and provides `admin?.role === 'admin'` to check permissions. Looking at ExerciseSetupPage (lines 22-23), this pattern is standard. **Admin-only buttons** like delete and repeater removal use conditional rendering on this check.

**Where EmHam Import Button Could Go:**
1. **Top header with Organisator field** (next to "Einrichten" and "Auswertung" buttons) — admin-only, styled like the existing buttons
2. **Within ExerciseSetupPage** — if import is tied to setup
3. **As a modal/dialog triggered from ExercisePage** — non-disruptive

The current button layout (lines 115-142) shows a flex row with Organisator input and two links. A file upload button could fit here, sized similarly to the existing buttons.

---

### 2. File Upload Patterns in Codebase

**Current State:**
- **NO existing file upload** in the entire codebase
- **NO `multer` dependency** in `server/package.json`
- Server uses `express.json({ limit: '1mb' })` (line 49 of `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/index.ts`)
- Client has no FormData usage in any component
- All API communication is JSON-only via `apiFetch()`

**Required Dependencies to Add:**
```json
"multer": "^1.4.5-lts.1"  // or latest
"pdf-parse": "^1.1.1"      // for PDF text extraction
```

Note: `pdfkit` is already in dependencies (used for PDF generation, not parsing).

---

### 3. Report Creation Flow

**POST Endpoint:** `/api/v1/exercises/:id/reports` (line 255 in `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`)

**Request Body Fields:**
```typescript
{
  operator_id: number,           // required
  repeater_id: number,           // required
  readability: number,           // RST readability (1-5), optional
  strength: number,              // RST strength (1-9), optional
  db_over_s9: string,            // e.g. "+30", "+60", optional
  einstiegspunkt_id: number,     // for linked repeaters, optional
  is_op_marker: boolean,         // marks OP position, optional, default 0
  notes: string,                 // optional
  bezirk_code: string,           // optional, auto-derived from operator if null
  suffix: string,                // e.g. "/m", "/p", optional
}
```

**Response** (line 281-290): Returns full report object with:
- `id, exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability, strength, db_over_s9, einstiegspunkt_id`
- `callsign, operator_name, operator_qth, bezirk_code, bundesland_code`
- `repeater_name, repeater_is_linked, einstiegspunkt_abbr, einstiegspunkt_name`

**Key Pattern:** Both LandMode and BundMode use `getOrCreateOperator()` (line 2 in `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/services/api.ts`):
```typescript
export async function getOrCreateOperator(callsign: string, existing: any, extras?: { name?: string; qth?: string }): Promise<any>
```
- If operator doesn't exist, POSTs to `/api/v1/operators` with `{ callsign, name?, qth? }`
- Returns operator object with `id` field
- Then submits report using that operator.id

**Server POST `/api/v1/operators`** (line 57 in `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/operators.ts`):
- Takes `{ callsign, name?, qth?, bezirk_code?, bundesland_code? }`
- Auto-derives `bundesland_code` from callsign prefix (OE8 → '08') via `callsignToCountryCode()`
- Auto-derives `bezirk_code` from QTH via `qthToBezirkCode()` if not provided
- Returns created operator with all fields

---

### 4. Repeater Matching Architecture

**Repeater Storage (Schema):**
File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts` lines 43-58

```sql
repeaters (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  short_name TEXT NOT NULL,           -- "HERMANNSKOGEL", "Gerlitzen 2m"
  site_name TEXT,
  band TEXT,                          -- "2m", "70cm", "23cm"
  callsign TEXT,                      -- repeater callsign (OE1XAT, etc.)
  frequency_mhz REAL,
  offset_mhz REAL,
  ctcss_hz REAL,
  burst_hz REAL,
  type TEXT DEFAULT 'repeater',       -- "repeater", "simplex", "linked"
  is_linked INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  sort_order INTEGER,
  bundesland_code TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code),
  is_custom INTEGER DEFAULT 0         -- user-created repeaters
)
```

**Exercise-Repeater Link:**
```sql
exercise_repeaters (
  exercise_id TEXT,                   -- UUID
  repeater_id INTEGER,
  operator_callsign TEXT,             -- OP for this repeater in this exercise
  home_repeater TEXT,                 -- per-exercise repeater assignment
  PRIMARY KEY (exercise_id, repeater_id)
)
```

**Matching Strategy for EmHam PDF:**
1. **Parse repeater name from PDF** (e.g., "HERMANNSKOGEL")
2. **Match to repeaters table** via `short_name` (case-insensitive fuzzy match recommended)
3. **Get repeater.id** and use in report creation
4. **Check if repeater is in exercise** via `exercise_repeaters` table
   - If not, either skip report or allow adding custom repeater (like ExerciseSetupPage does)

**Current Repeater List (~80 repeaters):**
From the excel-import-bericht.md (lines 39-53), the system has:
- 11 named repeaters (Gerlitzen 2m, Magdalensberg linked, etc.)
- Multiple simplex entries (Direkte 145.300, etc.)
- Linked repeaters map to einstiegspunkte (entry points)

**Example Matching:**
- PDF says "HERMANNSKOGEL" → match `repeaters.short_name LIKE '%HERMANNSKOGEL%'`
- Get `repeater_id = <id from DB>`
- Create report with that repeater_id

---

### 5. PDF Dependency Check

**Current Dependencies in `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/package.json`:**
```json
"pdfkit": "^0.17.2",           // ← PDF GENERATION only (for export)
"@types/pdfkit": "^0.17.5"
```

**Missing for PDF Import:**
- **`pdf-parse`** or similar parser NOT installed
- Need to add PDF text extraction library

**Recommended Approach:**
```json
"pdf-parse": "^1.1.1"  // Pure JS, works in Node/browser
```

Or alternatives:
- `pdfjs-dist`: Mozilla's PDF.js (heavier but industry standard)
- `pdf2json`: Converts PDF to JSON
- `pdfrw`: Lightweight, Python-like

For a simple text extraction from a tabular PDF (like EmHam), `pdf-parse` is sufficient.

---

### 6. Additional Architectural Notes

**Auth Middleware:**
File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/middleware/auth.ts`
- All `/api/v1/*` routes require JWT Bearer token (except `/auth/login`)
- `requireRole('admin')` enforces admin-only access
- Token payload includes `{ id, username, role }`

**Database Context:**
- SQLite via `better-sqlite3`
- DB instance obtained via `getDb()` singleton
- Transactions available via `db.transaction()`

**Socket Events:**
- Client-side: `getSocket()` from `client/src/services/socket.ts`
- Emits: `join_exercise`, `leave_exercise`, `report_created`, `report_updated`, `report_deleted`
- Server broadcasts via Socket.IO to all connected clients

**Error Handling Pattern:**
- API errors return JSON with `{ error: "message" }`
- Frontend catches via `apiFetch()` wrapper and shows error
- No stack traces in production (configured in index.ts line 80)

---

### 7. Key Database Columns for Import

When creating reports from EmHam PDF, populate these fields:

| Field | Source | Required | Notes |
|-------|--------|----------|-------|
| `operator_id` | PDF callsign | Yes | Create via getOrCreateOperator if not exists |
| `repeater_id` | PDF repeater name → match DB | Yes | Match against `repeaters.short_name` |
| `exercise_id` | Current exercise | Yes | UUID string (not integer) |
| `readability` | PDF RST digit 1 | No | 1-5 |
| `strength` | PDF RST digit 2 | No | 1-9 |
| `db_over_s9` | PDF RST suffix | No | "+30", "+60", etc. |
| `einstiegspunkt_id` | PDF location suffix | No | Only for linked repeaters, map via abbreviation |
| `bezirk_code` | PDF or callsign | No | Auto-filled from operator.bezirk_code if null |
| `notes` | PDF notes field | No | Store original PDF text or parsing notes |
| `entered_by` | "emham-import" | Yes | For audit trail |
| `suffix` | PDF operator suffix | No | "/m", "/p", etc. |

---

### 8. Example Report Creation (from LandMode)

File: `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/LandMode.tsx` lines 131-161 shows the pattern:

```typescript
const operator = await getOrCreateOperator(form.callsign, form.operator, { name: form.opName, qth: form.opQth });
const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);  // "5/9+30" → { readability: 5, strength: 9, db_over_s9: "+30" }

const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
  method: 'POST',
  body: JSON.stringify({
    operator_id: operator.id,
    repeater_id: repeaterId,
    ...parsed,
    notes: form.notes || null,
    bezirk_code: (bezirkCode && bezirkCode !== '??') ? bezirkCode : null,
    suffix: form.suffix || null,
  }),
});
```

For EmHam import, this same pattern applies but with bulk report creation.

---

### 9. UI/UX Placement Recommendation

**Option A: Header Button in ExercisePage**
```
[Organisator: OE0XXX] [Status] [Einrichten] [Auswertung] [📤 EmHam Import]
```
- Admin-only conditional render

**Option B: Integrated into ExerciseSetupPage**

**Option C: Separate admin utility page**

**Recommendation:** **Option A** (header button) for consistency with existing admin tools (Einrichten, Auswertung links).

### 10. Known Patterns to Follow

**Multi-transaction safety (from code):**
```typescript
const insertReportAndAttendance = db.transaction(() => {
  db.prepare(...).run(...);  // INSERT signal_reports
  db.prepare(...).run(...);  // INSERT exercise_attendance
  return result;
});
const result = insertReportAndAttendance();
```

**Operator creation with auto-detection:**
```typescript
const derivedBl = bundesland_code || callsignToCountryCode(callsign) || null;
const derivedBz = bezirk_code || (qth ? qthToBezirkCode(db, qth, derivedBl) : null);
```

**Repeater matching from Excel import (existing precedent):**
The excel-import-bericht.md shows the system already maps repeater names (lines 39-76) with fuzzy matching and manual fallback. EmHam import should use similar strategy.

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/.claude/plans/synchronous-enchanting-island.md",
  "content": "# Plan: EmHam PDF Import Feature\n\n## Context\nThe BOS-ARSA exercises are also logged in EmHam (by OE1VRS), which exports a structured PDF with callsign listings per Bundesland. We want to import these PDFs into BOS-ARSA Log to avoid double data entry. The PDF contains tabular data: Callsign, Name, QTH, BezKen, Locator, Rapport, Repeater, Notstrom, Info — grouped by Bundesland. All entries typically use one linked repeater (e.g. HERMANNSKOGEL) → BundMode.\n\n**Important: No deploy during this session — exercise is currently running.**\n\n## PDF Data Structure\nEach row: `Callsign | Name | QTH | BezKen | Locator | Rapport +Sig | Repeater | Notstrom | Info`\n- Rapport format: `5/8`, `5/9`, `5/9 10` (=+10dB), `5/9 60` (=+60dB)\n- Callsigns may have suffix: `OE1MVA / portabel`\n- Some fields can be empty (no Locator, no BezKen)\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. New server dependency: `pdf-parse`\n- `cd server && npm install pdf-parse`\n- Pure JS PDF text extraction, no native deps\n\n### 2. New server dependency: `multer`\n- `cd server && npm install multer @types/multer`\n- Multipart file upload middleware\n\n### 3. New server route: `server/src/routes/import.ts`\n- **POST `/api/v1/exercises/:id/import-emham`** — admin-only, multer single file upload\n- Steps:\n  1. Accept PDF file via multipart upload\n  2. Extract text with `pdf-parse`\n  3. Parse text into structured rows per Bundesland section\n  4. For each row:\n     - Get or create operator (callsign, name, QTH, bezirk_code)\n     - Match repeater by `short_name` against exercise's repeaters\n     - Parse rapport into readability/strength/db_over_s9\n     - Handle `/ portabel` → suffix `/p`\n  5. Return parsed data as JSON preview (don't insert yet)\n- **POST `/api/v1/exercises/:id/import-emham/confirm`** — admin-only\n  - Accept the parsed data array\n  - Create operators + signal_reports in a transaction\n  - Emit socket events for each created report\n  - Return summary (created count, skipped count)\n\n### 4. Register route: `server/src/index.ts`\n- Import and mount the new import router\n\n### 5. New client component: `client/src/components/EmHamImport.tsx`\n- Modal dialog triggered by \"EmHam Import\" button\n- UI flow:\n  1. File picker (accept `.pdf`)\n  2. Upload → show parsed preview table (callsign, name, QTH, bezirk, rapport, repeater match status)\n  3. Highlight any issues (unmatched repeater, missing data)\n  4. \"Importieren\" button → confirm endpoint → close modal, reports appear via socket\n- Admin-only (button only rendered for admin role)\n\n### 6. ExercisePage integration: `client/src/pages/ExercisePage.tsx`\n- Add admin-only \"EmHam Import\" button in the header row (next to Einrichten/Auswertung)\n- Button opens EmHamImport modal, passes `exerciseId` and exercise's repeaters list\n\n## Text Parsing Strategy\n```\nDetect sections: /^(OE\\d)\\s*-\\s*(.+)$/  →  bundesland header\nDetect rows: /^(OE\\d\\w+(?:\\s*\\/\\s*\\w+)?)\\s+(.+?)\\s+(\\d{4},\\s*.+?)\\s+(\\w{2})?\\s+(JN\\w+)?\\s+(\\d\\/\\d(?:\\s+\\d+)?)\\s+([\\w]+)\\s*/\nSkip: \"Gesamt:\", headers, stats pages, empty lines\n```\n\n## Field Mapping\n| PDF Column | Target | Handling |\n|---|---|---|\n| Callsign | `operators.callsign` | Strip `/ portabel` → suffix `/p` |\n| Vor-/Rufname | `operators.name` | Direct |\n| QTH | `operators.qth` | Direct |\n| BezKen | `signal_reports.bezirk_code` | Direct (WC, TU, GF, etc.) |\n| Locator | `signal_reports.notes` | Append to notes |\n| Rapport +Sig | readability/strength/db_over_s9 | Parse `5/9 10` → R=5, S=9, db=\"+10\" |\n| Repeater | `repeater_id` | Match `repeaters.short_name` in exercise |\n| Notstrom | `signal_reports.notes` | Append \"Notstrom\" to notes if checked |\n| Info | `signal_reports.notes` | Direct |\n\n## Files to modify/create\n- `server/package.json` — add `pdf-parse`, `multer`, `@types/multer`\n- `server/src/routes/import.ts` — **NEW** import route with parsing logic\n- `server/src/index.ts` — mount import router\n- `client/src/components/EmHamImport.tsx` — **NEW** modal component\n- `client/src/pages/ExercisePage.tsx` — add EmHam Import button\n\n## Verification\n1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run \"npm run build\"` compiles clean\n2. Upload the sample PDF → preview shows 17 parsed entries across OE1/OE3/OE4\n3. Confirm import → reports appear in BundMode\n4. Repeater \"HERMANNSKOGEL\" correctly matched to exercise repeater\n5. Rapport values parsed correctly (5/8, 5/9, 5/9+10, 5/9+60)\n6. Operators created with correct name, QTH, bezirk_code\n7. **No deploy** — exercise currently running\n"
}
